2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測(最終版) 
～前期比年率▲0.4％の小幅マイナス成長を予想～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

11 月16 日に公表される26 年７-９月期の実質
ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率▲0.4％（前期比▲
0.1％）と予測する。10 月 30 日の段階では前期
比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋0.1％）と小幅プラス
成長を予想していたが、その後公表された経済
指標の結果を反映し、小幅マイナス成長へと予
測値を下方修正する。 
本日公表された26 年９月分の国際収支統計の
結果を反映し、26 年７-９月期の実質輸出を前期
比＋0.1％（従来予測値：同＋1.2％）へ下方修
正する。筆者の９月分の想定と比べてサービス
輸出が大きく下振れたことが主因である。実質
輸入も前期比＋3.0％（従来予測値：同＋
3.3％）と下方修正するが、輸出の下方修正度合
いの方が大きいことから、外需の予測値を前期
比年率寄与度▲1.9％Pt（従来予測値：同▲
1.3％Pt）へ下方修正している。 
また、９月分の家計調査と家計消費状況調査
が想定よりも下振れたことを反映し、個人消費
の予測値も前期比▲0.3％と、従来予測値の同▲
0.2％から小幅下方修正した。

物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
ことが成長率を押し下げる見込みである。特に、８～９月には長雨・大雨や台風などの天候不順
が相次ぎ、外出手控えや旅行・宿泊の見送りなどを通じて消費が下押しされたとみられる。一
方、設備投資は高い伸びとなり、景気を下支えするとみられる。設備投資の伸びには特殊要因も

2/2
一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。
７-９月期は４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になると予想するが、マイナス幅は大きくない。今回
のマイナス成長には天候不順という一時的な下押し要因も含まれており、過去３四半期に高い成
長が続いた後であることや、企業部門の底堅さが維持されていることを踏まえれば、景気回復の
流れが途切れたと判断する必要はないだろう。
物価高による節約志向は根強く、個人消費の回復にはなお時間を要するとみられるが、設備投
資を中心に企業部門が好調さを維持している点は好材料である。家計部門が力強さを欠く一方、
企業部門が景気を下支えする構図のもと、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると判
断している。

崩れず～ | 新家 義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所（10 月 30 日発行）をご参照ください）

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
永續環境是漢唐經營理念之綠色承諾，因應全球環保節能的需求，我們透過
工程設計、系統整合提升工程效率，減少工程過程中能耗。漢唐集成響應政
府綠色採購辦法，我們從原物料取得、工程施作、運輸配送、完工至客戶使
用階段，優先要求廠商提供低污染、可回收、再利用之環保產品，以達到廢
棄物減量之目標。 
漢唐集成根著台灣，從初創之施工安裝小公司成長為專業工程集成公司，有
賴於各地政通人和，因此，漢唐集成本著感恩的心回饋在地，不斷捐助弱勢
及支持培育英才，期望肩擔起企業之「社會責任」，讓台灣變得更美好。漢
唐股東會由全體股東組成，對公司重大事項進行決策，定期聽取董事會報
告，為公司最高決策機關；董事會為最高治理機關，董事會成員皆恪盡善良
管理人之注意義務，擘劃公司的經營政策與檢視財務績效，並確保公司營運
遵守各種法令；為完善公司治理運作、強化公司競爭力，董事會設置審計委
員會與薪資報酬委員會來健全董事會運作；董事會之下亦設有獨立之稽核
室，定期執行稽核業務並向審計委員會與董事會呈報稽核結果。 
公司簡介： 
漢唐集成成立於 1982 年，為一以「全面性整合服務」方式，專業提供高科
技產業建廠之系統集成服務公司；主要業務範圍包括高科技廠房各系統，包
括電腦、控制、無塵室、機電、電信系統之施作與整合服務。 
漢唐目前在手訂單金額約 1,939 億元： 
漢唐 01~05/2026 年接單合約金額約 674 億元，截至 05/2026 年已簽約未認
列之金額(在手訂單)約 1,939 億元。在手訂單約 24~36 個月可以認列大部
分。目前在手訂單金額的施工地區，有 2/3 在台灣，1/3 在海外。 
24~36 個月認列大部分的在手訂單可能是保守估計，2H26 在手訂單金額可
能還會再增加。 
漢唐未來還有先進封裝廠的訂單： 
大客戶於美國建廠的第三期工程的訂單目前漢唐已經有接到一部份，還沒
有認列到手訂單金額中，客戶的先進封裝廠的訂單還沒發包。第二期工程進
度希望在 2026 年認列約 85%，第三期工程的認列狀況要看客戶，未來還有
先進封裝廠的訂單。 
美國營收已超過台灣： 
漢唐沒有透露前兩大客戶的營收占比。2025 年營收占比_地區別：台灣 65%、
美國 25%、其他 9%。 1Q26 台灣 40%、美國 56%、其他 4%。美系記憶體廠
在台灣蓋無塵室，漢唐還是主要的供應商。 
漢唐對營運樂觀： 
漢唐表示目前建廠需求遠大於供給，因目前人力短缺，尤其是在台灣，暫時
沒辦法去接其他大型客戶的訂單。由於 AI 與相關需求，現在客戶建廠的需
求會延續到 2029 年，漢唐對整體產業的展望表示樂觀。

                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

3 群益投顧 
其他補充： 
漢唐的費用比較固定，管理費用包含員工酬勞。漢唐追求與客戶長期合作，
如果是原物料上漲導致成本增加，客戶可以接受漲價。 
目前漢唐在全球的員工人數約 1,700 人，美國、台灣都會再增加人力，之後
也是要看下包廠商，看是否能擴大供應鏈。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

美国科技股遭遇新冠疫情以来最惨烈⼀周，纳斯达克 100 指数跌⼊熊市，本周科技股市值蒸
发逾 4 万亿美元。 Anthropic 上市⾸⽇涨幅迅速回落，英伟达因财务丑闻单⽇暴跌 14% ，拖
累芯⽚板块崩盘。恐慌指数 VIX ⼀度飙升⾄ 40 点，⽐特币跌破 6 万美元，⻩⾦下挫近 3% ，
投资者涌⼊美债避险， 10 年期收益率降⾄ 4.94% 附近。
摘要：
美国科技股遭遇新冠疫情暴发以来最惨烈的⼀周， AI 投资热潮降温引发的恐慌情绪席卷华尔
街。
周五，纳斯达克 100 指数单⽇重挫 5.6% ，当周累计下跌 10.4% ，创逾六年来最⼤单周跌幅，较 10
⽉ 6 ⽇盘中⾼点累计下跌已逾 20% ，正式步⼊熊市区间。本周科技股市值蒸发逾 4 万亿美元。
到周五收盘，纳指抹平今年内累计涨幅转跌
标普 500 指数同期下跌 3.7% ，较 10 ⽉⾼点回调超过 10% ，进⼊技术性调整区域。
⽆论是业绩和指引超预期强劲的英伟达财报，还是 Anthropic 创纪录的 IPO 募资，本周都未能拯

救美股

### Page 2

与此同时， Anthropic 上市⾸⽇⼤涨 17% 的喜悦昙花⼀现，次⽇即告回落，这家 AI 明星公司的
IPO ⾮但未能提振市场信⼼，反⽽成为本轮抛售加速的导⽕索之⼀。
市场恐慌情绪迅速蔓延⾄⻛险资产全线。华尔街" 恐慌指数 "VIX⼀度飙升⾄ 40 点，创 2025 年 4 ⽉
以来最⾼⽔平；⽐特币跌破 6 万美元，创两年新低；⻩⾦现货价格下挫近 3% ⾄每⾦衡盎司约
3522 美元，触及逾⼀年低位；
本周，⻩⾦暴跌 9%
投资者争相涌⼊避险资产， 10 年期美国国债收益率下⾏ 10 个基点⾄ 4.94% 。
10 年期美国国债收益率下⾏⾄ 5% 下⽅
纳斯达克 100 创六年最差单周表现，正式步⼊熊市
据英国《⾦融时报》报道，纳斯达克 100 指数周五单⽇重挫 5.6% ，周跌幅扩⼤⾄ 10.4% ，为逾六
年来最差单周表现。该指数较 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇盘中⾼点累计下跌 20% ，正式触及熊市技术⻔槛。

### Page 3

标普 500 指数周五下跌 3.7% ，⾃ 10 ⽉峰值以来跌幅已超 10% ，进⼊调整区间，⾃ 4 ⽉份以来⾸次
收于 200 ⽇移动平均线以下。道琼斯⼯业平均指数本周跌幅达 8% 。
据华尔街⽇报报道，本周纳斯达克综合指数成分股合计蒸发市值逾 4 万亿美元，该指数今年以来
的全部涨幅已告抹平。 " 七⼤科技巨头 " ⽆⼀幸免，新兴云计算公司和芯⽚制造商普遍出现两位数
跌幅。

### Page 4

这场抛售的根源在于市场对 AI 投资狂潮可持续性的深层质疑。⼤型科技公司为建设芯⽚和数据
中⼼⽽⼤举举债投⼊，但 AI 相关企业的营收增速正在放缓。
据彭博智库数据， AI 企业在标普 500 中的销售增速预计将从第三季度的 35% 降⾄明年底的 25% 以
下，⽽其现⾦流与资本⽀出之⽐已跌⾄三年前的约五分之⼀。
据摩根⼠丹利投资管理⾸席投资官 Jim Caron 判断，⺠主党在本⽉中期选举中的⼤胜 " 绝对 " 是加
速这轮市场抛售的因素之⼀。他说：
在货币政策层⾯，美联储的加息预期持续压制市场情绪。美联储 9 ⽉已时隔三年重启加息，市场
原本对 12 ⽉再次加息 25 个基点的预期出现动摇，但美联储理事 Christopher Waller 此前曾放⻛⼀
次性加息 50 个基点。
Nomura 美洲股票联席主管 Alex Witten 指出：
Anthropic IPO 次⽇⼀度跌超 10% ， AI 明星上市也没能救市
周四上市的 Anthropic （ ANT ）当天收涨 17.1% ，⼀度较 83 美元的发⾏价上涨近四成，成为今年

全球资本市场最受瞩⽬的 IPO 之⼀。但上市次⽇，股价便⼤幅回落：周五收跌 8.5% ，盘中⼀度
跌⾄ 83.05 美元，⽇内跌幅超过 14% ，距离 IPO 发⾏价仅差 5 美分，险些破发。
Anthropic 股价的迅速回落，成为 AI 交易情绪转变的⼀个缩影。市场此前期待这家明星 AI 公司的
上市能够重新点燃投资者对 AI 板块的热情，但 IPO ⾸⽇的上涨并未带来持续的板块买盘。相反，
随着 Anthropic 次⽇转跌，英伟达、 AMD 及其他 AI 相关股票同步下挫。
与 AI 资本开⽀相关的所有⽀出 —— 我们知道正是这些⽀出推动了增⻓ —— 那么，这些⽀出是否会
被新⼀届国会明年就任后启动的调查和政策所压制？市场正在努⼒搞清楚这⼀点。
股市⼀直聚焦于英伟达丑闻，但现在我们同时⾯临美联储准备加息以及⼤宗商品市场反映的衰退
⻛险。

### Page 5

媒体指出， Anthropic 上市与英伟达最新季度业绩都未能打消市场对 AI ⻛险和巨额投资的担忧。
Horizon Investment Services ⾸席执⾏官 Chuck Carlson 表⽰：
这番话点出了市场的核⼼变化：投资者并⾮不相信 AI 技术前景，⽽是开始质疑当前估值是否已经
充分计⼊未来增⻓。 Anthropic 上市⾸⽇的热度没能延续，意味着即使是最受追捧的 AI 公司，也
未必能在当前市场环境中获得持续的估值溢价。
更深层的担忧在于 AI 商业化和资本开⽀之间的落差。⼤型科技公司过去⼀年投⼊巨额资⾦建设数
据中⼼、采购芯⽚和扩充算⼒，但投资者越来越关注这些投⼊何时能够转化为⾜以覆盖成本的
收⼊和利润。 AI 需求仍在增⻓，并不意味着所有相关企业都能获得与投资规模相匹配的回报。
英伟达七连跌， SEC 会计调查再添压⼒，市值较巅峰蒸发 2.65 万亿
英伟达（ NVDA ）周五收跌 14% ，本周累跌 22.92% ，创 2008 年 10 ⽉以来最⼤单周跌幅。

⾃ CEO ⻩仁勋 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇上周四离任后，英伟达股价已连续七个交易⽇下跌，也未能扭转跌势七
⽇累计跌幅达 27.74% 。即使本周⼆盘后英伟达公布的第三财季营收和第四财季指引均超预期强
劲，该司⾼管还上调了下个财年的营收增⻓指引，也未能扭转股价跌势。
截⾄周五收盘，英伟达市值较 10 ⽉接近 6 万亿美元的峰值蒸发约 2.65 万亿美元，苹果（ AAPL ）、
微软（ MSFT ）和⾕歌⺟公司 Alphabet （ GOOGL ）均已超过英伟达，后者退居美国上市公司市
值第四位。
“ 英伟达（本周）公布的业绩可能是企业盈利史上最出⾊的季度表现之⼀，他们把所有事情都做
对了。我和许多投资者⼀样，原本在某种程度上期待 Anthropic 上市能够成为⼀次市场情绪的出
清事件，但它并没有起到这样的作⽤。 ”

### Page 6

英伟达⾯临的压⼒，已不仅仅是整个 AI 板块估值回调。⻩仁勋离任后，公司由⾸席财务官
（ CFO ）克雷斯（ Colette Kress ）临时兼任 CEO ，管理层更迭本⾝就令投资者对公司未来战略
和经营稳定性产⽣疑虑。与此同时，围绕⻩仁勋个⼈婚外情及其关联⼈⼠的调查报道，也持续
给公司带来声誉和监管层⾯的不确定性。
监管压⼒更在本周进⼀步升级。《华尔街⽇报》周⼆报道称，美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）正在
调查英伟达，但当时尚⽆法确定调查的具体重点。路透社周五进⼀步援引两名知情⼈⼠报道
称， SEC 执法部⻔正在仔细审查英伟达可能存在的会计问题。
路透社称，调查关注点之⼀是英伟达此前调整地理区域收⼊披露⼝径：公司改为按照客⼾总部
所在地，⽽⾮账单地址统计收⼊，调整后新加坡不再单独出现在地区收⼊披露中。与此同时，
市场也在关注英伟达参与的复杂 AI 融资安排 —— 芯⽚⼚商通过投资、担保等⽅式帮助客⼾获得资
⾦，以购买芯⽚和建设数据中⼼。这类安排可能令投资者更难判断收⼊质量和公司实际承担的

财务⻛险。
需要强调的是， SEC 调查并不代表英伟达已经存在违规⾏为，路透社也表⽰⽬前尚⽆法确认调
查何时启动、具体审查哪些会计事项。不过，监管调查在股价已经⼤幅下跌、管理层刚刚发⽣
变动之际传出，⽆疑进⼀步打击了投资者信⼼。
路透社报道发布前，英伟达股价跌幅已超过 6% ，报道传出后，股价跌幅扩⼤⾄ 10% 以上。
沽空报告后 AMD 连续两⽇重挫， AI 产业链遭全⾯抛售
半导体板块的抛售也在向其他公司扩散。 AMD （ AMD ）周四在沽空机构 Hunterbrook 发布报告
后收跌 7.7% ，周五进⼀步下跌逾 13% ，连续第⼆个交易⽇⼤幅下挫。费城半导体指数周五收跌
约 10.7% ，反映市场抛售已从个别公司扩散⾄整个芯⽚板块。
其他 AI 产业链股票同样承压。美光（ MU ）周五跌约 14% ，博通（ AVGO ）跌逾 12% ；云算⼒服
务 商 CoreWeave （ CRWV ） 和 Nebius 分 别 下 跌 约 12% 和 16% 。 ⼤ 型 科 技 公 司 中 ， Meta

（ META ）跌约 5.9% ，亚⻢逊（ AMZN ）跌约 4.1% 。
AI 基础设施投资链条也受到波及。卡特彼勒（ CAT ）周五下跌约 5% ，市场担忧如果数据中⼼建
设热潮降温，发电和备⽤电⼒设备需求也可能受到影响。换⾔之，市场正在重新评估的不只是
芯⽚⼚商的估值，还包括数据中⼼建设、电⼒供应和云算⼒租赁等⼀整条 AI 投资链的增⻓预期。
AI 资本开⽀回报遭质疑，拥挤交易平仓放⼤跌势

### Page 7

这轮抛售最重要的深层原因，是市场对 AI 投资回报周期的重新审视。过去，投资者将 AI 视为⼀场
⾜以重塑经济和企业盈利的⻓期技术⾰命，愿意提前为算⼒需求和数据中⼼建设⽀付⾼估值。
但随着全球科技巨头不断提⾼资本开⽀，市场开始追问：这些投⼊究竟能够创造多少新增收
⼊？利润何时兑现？如果商业化进度不及预期，庞⼤的算⼒基础设施⼜将由谁来承担成本？
彭博社将这轮⾏情描述为 AI 交易的快速重新定价：投资者开始重新评估数万亿美元 AI 投资未来可
能带来的利润，⽽不再单纯追逐资本开⽀增⻓本⾝。 Anthropic 上市次⽇回落，也让市场意识
到，单个 AI 企业获得巨额融资和⾼估值，并不能证明整个⾏业的投资逻辑已经得到验证。
与此同时， AI 相关股票此前涨幅巨⼤，交易也相对拥挤。当英伟达等⻰头股持续下跌，投资者可
能被迫削减相关仓位、降低杠杆，进⽽引发更多抛售。半导体指数、云算⼒服务商和⼤型科技
股同步⼤跌，正说明这已不是个别公司的基本⾯调整，⽽是市场整体降低 AI ⻛险敞⼝的过程。
美国银⾏财富管理公司⾼级投资总监 Bill Northey 表⽰：

加息押注升温，估值压⼒与避险情绪形成共振
宏观环境也在放⼤ AI 股票的压⼒。美国通胀数据⾼于预期后，市场对美联储 12 ⽉会议加息 25 个
基点的押注升⾄接近确定。美联储理事库克（ Lisa Cook ）周五表⽰，当前政策利率还不⾜以应
对通胀⽔平。下周公布的美联储⻘睐通胀指标个⼈消费⽀出（ PCE ）数据，将成为市场判断联
储政策路径的重要依据。
利率预期变化对 AI 股票尤其不利。 AI 企业的⾼估值很⼤程度上建⽴在未来多年收⼊和利润快速增
⻓的预期之上；利率越⾼，远期现⾦流折现价值越低，投资者愿意为尚未兑现的增⻓⽀付的价
格也就越低。同时，数据中⼼建设和算⼒扩张⾼度依赖融资，融资成本上升也会增加 AI 投资项⽬
的资⾦压⼒。
周五，美股⼤跌伴随美债收益率明显回落， 10 年期美债收益率⽇内降约 11 个基点⾄ 4.94% 附
近，显⽰资⾦正在转向相对安全的资产。 VIX 恐慌指数⼤幅上升，市场避险情绪进⼀步升温。
“ 但如果我们退⼀步，从基本⾯和市场情绪两个⻆度审视，今天显然是市场情绪占据了上⻛。 ”

“ 短期内，情绪始终是更有⼒量的驱动因素；但从更⻓时间跨度看，最终占上⻛的通常还是基本
⾯。 ”

### Page 8

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

和监管不确定性加剧， AMD 等芯⽚股接连遭遇个股冲击，⽽美联储加息预期⼜令⾼估值资产更

公司簡介：旺矽成立於 1995 年，為全球前五大探針卡廠，主要從事半導體測試板
的設計與生產，品項包括晶圓測試(CP)的懸臂式探針卡（Cantilever Probe Cards, 
CPC）、垂直式探針卡（Vertical Probe Cards, VPC）、微機電探針卡(Micro Electro 
Mechanical Systems, MEMS)、探針卡 PCB、中介層載板(interposer/substrate)
等；半導體設備製造，包含光電自動化設備(Photonics Automation, PA)、溫度測
試設備(Thermal Test, Thermal) 、先進半導體測試設備(Advanced 
Semiconductor Test, AST)。其他業務包含各子公司營收，長洛國際(銷售母公司
旺矽機台設備)、琉明光電(LED 晶粒代工)、Celadon Systems(2021 年 8 月併購，
生產 AST 工程測試探針卡)。客戶包含全球各大 IC 設計公司、晶圓代工廠、封測
廠。3Q26 產品比重：探針卡 72.7%、半導體設備 25.4%、其他業務 1.9%。 
3Q26 毛利率優於預期：旺矽 3Q26 營收 59.04 億元(+12.8%QoQ，+72.9%YoY)，
毛利率 61.7%，優於研究部預期 61.2%，主要受惠於 HPC 探針卡營收成長強勁，
產品組合轉佳，營益率 38.6%，稅後淨利 18.80 億元(+23.3%QoQ，+114.4%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 19.18 元。 
4Q26 營收維持季增趨勢：展望 4Q26，公司預期營收有望季增 5~10%，惟考量
AI ASIC 探針卡客戶持續放量出貨、Non-AI ASIC 探針卡客戶於 4Q26 開始貢獻，
以及半導體設備營收傾向年底認列，加上匯率維持有利，研究部預期營運表現有望
優於公司展望，預估 4Q26 營收 69.90 億元（+18.4%QoQ，+82.2%YoY），毛利
率 62.1%，營益率 40.7%，稅後淨利 23.54 億元（+25.2%QoQ，+148.4%YoY），

Document 201: 8月美国CPI数据解读及9月FOMC前瞻：偏鹰数据与鸽派加息预期

Source PDF: all report pdf/8月美国CPI数据解读及9月FOMC前瞻：偏鹰数据与鸽派加息预期.pdf
Report source: 南华期货
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

.
偏鹰数据与鸽派加息预期
——8月美国CPI数据解读及9月FOMC前瞻
高翔（投资咨询证号： Z0016413 ）
潘响（投资咨询证号： Z0021448 ）
联系邮箱： panxiang@nawaa.com
交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【 2011 】 1290 号
2026 年 9 月 13 日
摘要：在本次数据发布前，市场已经就“核心CPI”是本次唯一关注焦点达成共识。本次核心CPI环比超出预
期的表现实际上是一个偏鹰的结果，这也导致数据发布后市场9月加息预期蹿升。但另一方面，其他大类资产
的表现却显得不够鹰派，周五夜盘的走势呈现出明显的加息前置交易特征，这也是目前市场普遍认为本次会
议可能呈现出“鸽派加息”的原因。推高核心通胀超预期的因素完全集中于核心服务项的异常跳升，主要包
括无线电话服务、酒店住宿，以及受油价传导影响的机票价格。拉动本次核心CPI环比上行的上述关键分项大
概率不具备持续性，剔除上述高波动、一次性扰动因素后，美国底层粘性通胀仍在温和降温，通胀向2%目标
收敛的内生趋势并未被破坏。

9月FOMC前瞻：鸽派加息之后就是一路坦途吗？
1）加息前置交易与鸽派加息预期。我们认为本次会议可能是开启加息的最佳时间窗口，市场的走势也表现出
来了对本次加息的预期。
2）可能情景猜想。最理想情况下，若9月FOMC会议在加息一次的情况下，给出继续观望或者再多一次左右
的加息预期，会出现对市场稳定以及风险偏好回升最为友好的情况。 但我们认为实际情况可能不会如此乐
观，加息落地后大概率进入到新的观察周期，短期内风险资产压力减弱，但依旧会受到地缘以及后续数据公
布情况的影响。
而如果本次会议依旧保持利率水平不变，那并不意味着通胀压力可控，反而可能导向最坏的局面。即便沃什
能够说服其他委员同事放弃抬高利率，从过去两次FOMC会议后的市场表现来看，市场会通过远端利率进一
步的冲高来预防通胀脱锚的风险。
1、为什么本次通胀数据是决定性的？
在本次数据发布前，市场已经就“核心CPI”是本次唯一关注焦点达成共识。理由在于面对超长端利率失控以
及市场愈发高涨的通胀担忧下，沃什在杰克逊霍尔央行年会上指出当前劳动力市场符合充分就业，美联储当

前的首要关注应当是物价。“必须确信潜在通胀正在向我们的目标移动——清晰地、以足够的速度。否则，
我们就还有工作要做。”除此之外，联储理事沃勒在公开讲话时给出了十分清晰的阈值——核心CPI环比
0.2%-0.3%。0.2%这一门槛之所以至关重要，是因为该水平能够确认通胀放缓的实质性趋势。若核心CPI环

比能够稳定在0.2%及以内，意味着该指标将连续4个月处于0.2%以下区间，近7个月平均增速约0.2%（对应
同比约2.4%）。这可以基本确立通胀放缓的趋势，证明该下行信号并非单纯来自季节性扰动。而本次核心
CPI的超预期上行，是推动市场美联储加息预期显著升温的重要因素。
因此本次核心CPI环比超出预期的表现实际上是一个偏鹰的结果，这也导致数据发布后市场9月加息预期蹿
升。但另一方面，其他大类资产的表现却显得不够鹰派，周五夜盘的走势呈现出明显的加息前置交易特征，
这也是目前市场普遍认为本次会议可能呈现出“鸽派加息”的原因。
CPI 数据公布前后市场加息预期
数据来源：彭博  南华研究
2、数据解读
2026年8月美国CPI数据呈现“表观反弹、核心降温”的结构性特征。整体CPI环比升至0.4%（前值0.1%），
同比持平于3.4%；核心CPI环比略超预期升至0.3%（前值0.2%），但同比继续回落至2.4%（前值2.5%）。
本次通胀数据基本符合市场预期，但更关键的是核心CPI环比略超市场预期，这意味着当前核心通胀是否存在

反弹失速的风险吗？
1）推高核心通胀超预期的因素完全集中于核心服务项的异常跳升，主要包括无线电话服务、酒店住宿，以及
受油价传导影响的机票价格。无线电话服务是本月最大的单项拉动项，环比大幅飙升至5.9%，创下历史极
值，该项对核心CPI环比的增量贡献达0.08个百分点左右。外宿分项环比由7月的-3.4%大幅反弹至0.5%，直
接带动整体住房项环比升至0.3%。运输服务中的机票分项受航空燃油价格暴涨直接冲击，机票价格环比跳
涨，带动运输服务环比上行至0.5%。

2）拉动本次核心CPI环比上行的上述关键分项大概率不具备持续性。电话、酒店分项的跳升属于暂时性扰
动：无线电话服务的极端上涨，主要源于美国劳工统计局（BLS）调整统计方法（新算法对全行业套餐调价更
敏感），叠加运营商集中提价，属于典型一次性扰动。酒店住宿价格反弹，主要反映暑期出行需求带来的季
节性扰动。而作为决定通胀中长期走势的核心基石，粘性住房租金已经出现改善。主要居所租金和业主等价
租金环比均从0.3%放缓至0.2%。
综上，剔除上述高波动、一次性扰动因素后，美国底层粘性通胀仍在温和降温，通胀向2%目标收敛的内生趋
势并未被破坏。
CPI 数据整体表现情况（ % ）
数据来源：同花顺  南华研究
3、9月FOMC前瞻：鸽派加息之后就是一路坦途吗？
1）加息前置交易与鸽派加息预期
我们认为本次会议可能是开启加息的最佳时间窗口。首先，目前通胀最大的外生变量来自油价和地缘局势，
而这并不是货币财政政策能够干预的范畴，且由于美国政府高度的不确定性，其未来的走势也具有高度的不

可预测性，这会对通胀带来持续性的压力。其次，长端利率持续上行正在成为风险资产的压力，而缓解这一

### Page 4

压力最好的办法或许是加息。最后则回到联储，在8月杰克逊霍尔年会后，当市场观察到通胀数据表现出脱离
下行趋势的风险时，投资者期待美联储能够履行其政策框架以恢复联储的信誉。
我们认为市场的走势也表现出来了对本次加息的预期。本轮数据发布后，在加息预期显著升温的背景下，美
元指数、美债收益率均走出先上行后快速回落的行情。以10年期美债收益率为例，周五晚间偏鹰数据落地瞬
间，利率直线冲高，但随后又立马180度折返向下，这主要是因为市场事前对于9月加息的计价可能已经比较
充分（周四PPI后利率总体上行10bp以上）。如前文所说，这是典型的加息前置交易特征——更早的加息时
点、更小的加息幅度、更平稳的风险偏好。而上述对于本轮加息的高接受度最终指向了一次鸽派加息落地的
联储会议，即投资者预期本次会议在如期宣布进行加息25bp外，同时可能会伴随出现一份不太激进的政策声
明或是点阵图。
数据公布后， 10Y 美债收益率表现情况（ % ）
数据来源：同花顺  南华研究
2 ）可能情景猜想
最理想情况下，若 9 月 FOMC 会议在加息一次的情况下，给出继续观望或者再多一次左右的加息预期，会出现

对市场稳定以及风险偏好回升最为友好的情况。因为其一方面消除了市场对于联储的质疑，有助于降低通胀
的不确定性，也把加息预期控制在市场可以接受的程度内。从 FED watch 的年内加息预期来看，年内加息 2 次
的概率大概在 70% 左右，而从目前 1y1y 远期利率对 1 年期美债利率的利差来看，短端利率计价了 2 次的降息，
而目前海外投行预期加息次数的众数也在 2 次，总体来说资产的计价已经相对充分。
但我们认为实际情况可能不会如此乐观，加息落地后大概率进入到新的观察周期，短期内风险资产压力减
弱，但依旧会受到地缘以及后续数据公布情况的影响。首先，根据历史经验，美联储利率周期从降息转向加
息后基本会进行多次加息，仅进行一次加息又再度转向降息的案例出现在 1997 年，原因是亚洲金融危机的爆
发。其次，根据沃什本人表态，目前的政策利率水平并不处在限制性的区间，而一次 25bp 的加息或许并不足
够。
而如果本次会议依旧保持利率水平不变，那并不意味着通胀压力可控，反而可能导向最坏的局面。即便沃什

能够说服其他委员同事放弃抬高利率，从过去两次 FOMC 会议后的市场表现来看，市场会通过远端利率进一
步的冲高来预防通胀脱锚的风险。

SK하이닉스 (000660)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

Document 290: Capital Market Outlook (2026-11-02)

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook
No
vember 2, 2026
All data, projections and opinions are as of the date of this report and subject to change.
IN THIS ISSUE
Macro Strategy—Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do
About It: As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably
above $6 per gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major
theme of 2026. Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event,

on track to be one of the strongest on record. The upshot: more extreme weather
from droughts to heavy rains, warmer temperatures globally, lower crop production in
commodity-exporting economies, and higher prices across major agricultural products.
Importantly, these pressures won’t be equally distributed and will largely depend on the
magnitude and timing of weather events. Among the most vulnerable will be heavily
import-dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa and parts of Asia, while developed

economies that count food as a smaller share of consumer spending should be better
insulated. For investors, the prospect of a very strong El Niño only further reinforces our
conviction in several structural trends including a return to higher inflation and higher
interest rates, elevated commodity prices amid heightened geopolitical risk and supply
chain shortages, and greater investment in energy infrastructure and supply chain
resilience. In portfolios, we favor commodities as both an inflation hedge and diversifier.

Market View—Another Tale of Market Rotation: October offered another reminder
that headline index returns do not always tell the full story. The S&P 500 ended the month
slightly lower, but the more muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market
leadership beneath the surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the
Artificial Intelligence (AI) trade, particularly across semiconductors and memory stocks. Yet
pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S. Equities.

Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market, with Value-
oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. While it remains too early to
determine whether the shift will prove durable, October’s rotation may reflect less a retreat
from the AI theme and more a recalibration of valuations and expected returns as market
leadership broadens. For investors, the speed of the shift reinforces the importance of
diversification as the bull market enters a potentially more mature and differentiated phase.

Thought of the Week—Reorienting Around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction
to Capital Goods: AI is reshaping the physical economy, and companies are reorienting
capacity and investment plans to meet surging demand for data centers, power and
infrastructure. Industrial firms are adding or reviving capacity, while data center construction
has increased more than 60% over the past year and is outpacing other major construction
categories. At the same time, robust capital goods orders and long lead times for inputs such

as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers highlight both the strength of demand
and the supply constraints the buildout faces. Taking stock of these signposts, our view
is reinforced that the AI investment cycle remains in its middle innings, creating potential
opportunities for companies supplying the equipment and infrastructure behind the buildout.

A series of macro shocks, including
higher energy prices, rising Treasury
yields and shifting Federal Reserve
expectations, have tested markets,
but resilient economic growth, strong
corporate earnings and ongoing
artificial intelligence-related investment

continue to support a constructive
outlook for Equities and risk assets.
We remain overweight Equities, favor
U.S. stocks, see potential opportunities
in broader market participation and
select cyclical sectors, and view higher
bond yields as attractive to add income
and extend duration.

MACRO STRATEGY
Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do About It
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably above $6 per
gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major theme of 2026.
Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event, on track to be one

of the strongest on record. Below we answer frequently asked questions on El Niño,
the potential impact on the global economy and markets and, most importantly, how
investors can position portfolios accordingly.
What is El Niño, and what makes this time different? El Niño is the warm phase
of the “El Niño-Southern Oscillation,” a natural climate pattern occurring every two to
seven years. Simply put, El Niño refers to the warming of sea surface temperatures in

the eastern Pacific, historically leading to drier conditions in some areas (Southeast Asia,
Australia, Central America, southern Africa) and wetter conditions in others (southern
U.S., parts of Latin America) (Exhibit 1).
What makes this episode different? Should forecasts prove correct, this El Niño is on
track to be historic. The National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration’s latest
outlook assigns at least an 83% probability that El Niño’s strength during October-

December will exceed that of any previous episode since 1950. Already, its impact has
been felt via wildfires in Indonesia, the weakest monsoon season in India since 2015,1
and severe flooding from Paraguay to Peru. The upshot: more extreme weather, hotter
temperatures globally, lower crop production in commodity-exporting economies, and
higher prices across major agricultural products.
Exhibit 1: El Niño's Impact Varies Globally.
Geography Typical rainfall pattern Specific impacts

South Asia Weaker summer monsoon risk Lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation
Southeast Asia Drier Lower rice and palm oil production, wildfire risk, food inflation
United States Wetter in the south; warmer/drier in the north Southern flood risk; lower northern heating demand
Central America & Northern South America Drier Drought, lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation, lower hydropower output
Southern Africa Drier Weaker maize harvests, food inflation, lower hydropower output

Western South America Wetter along the Pacific coast Flooding, infrastructure damage, disrupted fisheries
Southern South America Wetter Flooding and crop damage; potential yield gains in some areas
Australia Drier Drought, agricultural losses, elevated wildfire risk, food inflation
Source: National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration, World Meteorological Organization, Food and Agriculture Organization, Australian Bureau of Meteorology, World Bank. Data as of

October 30, 2026.
How will El Niño impact the global economy and markets? Which regions will be
most affected? El Niño’s impact on the global economy and markets is likely to be felt
most via commodity price inflation. Alongside higher energy and fertilizer costs resulting
from war in Europe and the Middle East, El Niño could put further upward pressure on
commodities like wheat, sugar, corn, soy, cocoa and coffee as drought (or extreme rain)
weighs on crop yields and production. Latest estimates from the United Nations Food

and Agriculture Organization (FAO) suggest global food prices were up 5.8% year-over-
year (YoY) in September (Exhibit 2). Livestock is also likely to be challenged by higher
temperatures globally, while flooding has already threatened copper production in
Chile. Meanwhile, with hydropower accounting for more than half of the average annual
electricity generation in Brazil, Colombia, Peru and other Latin American countries,2
below-average rainfall in affected areas has huge implications for electricity generation.

As an example, Colombia’s natural gas demand nearly doubled during the 2023-2024 El
Niño event.2 Another key watchpoint will be whether drier conditions reduce water levels
1 India Meteorological Department, Bloomberg. Refers to rainfall between June and September as of September 30, 2026.
2 International Energy Agency, as of October 7, 2026
Investment Implications
We expect El Niño to place upward
pressure on agricultural goods
prices globally. We continue to

see commodities as a strategic
diversifier and hedge against inflation
in portfolios. The U.S. remains our
preferred region with companies in
Industrials and Materials as potential
beneficiaries. Meanwhile, economic
impacts to the emerging world will vary
by country but the broader Emerging
Market (EM) equity market could be
less exposed given its significant tilt
toward Technology.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
in the Panama Canal and other key shipping routes, particularly in light of the Strait of
Hormuz closure.
These pressures won’t be equally distributed and will heavily depend on this El Niño’s
severity. Among the more vulnerable to higher agricultural prices will be heavily import-
dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa, and parts of Asia. So too will economies
that count food as a larger share of consumer spending such as the Philippines (36%),

Indonesia (33%), Egypt (33%), India (28%), and Vietnam (26%) (Exhibit 3). Developed
economies like the U.S. (6%), Canada (10%), and Germany (11%) stand to be better
insulated from a food-related shock to headline inflation.
Within the U.S., some impacts could be positive with a potentially milder winter reducing
heating costs and wetter conditions in the South improving agricultural conditions. A recent
paper from the Federal Reserve Bank of San Francisco found that, in addition to putting

upward pressure on personal consumption expenditures (PCE) inflation, strong El Niño
episodes have been historically associated with a temporary boost to employment in the
following year via better construction conditions in the North and weather-event recovery
efforts.3 In California and parts of the Southwest, El Niño will be yet another stress test for
property insurers as flooding and landslide risks increase.
For more vulnerable developing economies, meanwhile, higher food and energy costs could

present a policy dilemma. On one hand, governments in vulnerable areas may move to
introduce fiscal relief to support households and boost growth. History points to plenty of
examples of this, such as Indonesia’s cash assistance in late 2023 or Thailand’s $1.8 billion
in loans to farmers in 2015. On the other hand, elevated inflation expectations and, in
some cases, weaker currencies could limit EM central banks’ room to ease monetary policy,
particularly with several major developed central banks aligned in hiking interest rates. Also

complicating matters: Global sovereign debt surpassed $110 trillion in Q2 2026, leaving
less fiscal space to absorb incoming shocks.4
How can investors position portfolios accordingly? In many ways, the prospect of a
very strong El Niño only reinforces structural trends we’ve identified as defining the coming
years: a return to higher inflation and higher interest rates; elevated commodity prices
amid heightened geopolitical risk and resource/supply shortages; and greater investment in

energy infrastructure and supply chain resilience.
Considering these themes, we continue to like commodities not just as a hedge against
higher inflation but also as a diversification tool in portfolios given their negative correlation
with Equities and bonds. That’s not to mention secular forces placing upward pressure on
commodity prices such as the resource-intensive AI buildout and broader protectionism. To
wit, the Bloomberg Commodity Index, which spans oil, gold, various metals and agricultural

goods has gained nearly 30% this year, handily outperforming the S&P 500 (+1%) and the
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index (-1%).
Within Equities, the U.S. is still our preferred region. While upside risk to inflation remains
a risk to watch going into 2027, food is a relatively small share of the consumer basket in
the U.S., and neither strong services spending nor capital investment should be materially
affected by El Niño. Companies within the Industrials and Materials sectors focused on

precision agriculture, water management and crop protection may be positioned to benefit.
Import-dependent EM economies remain particularly exposed, though impacts will vary
widely depending on the magnitude and timing of weather events. It’s worth noting too that
EM Equities remain heavily concentrated in East Asian markets with substantial exposure to
technology, leaving the broader index arguably less exposed to the economic ramifications
of El Niño than in past episodes. Indeed, even after recent weakness in semiconductor

names, Taiwan, Korea, and China together account for a near-70% of the broader MSCI
Emerging Markets Index.5 We maintain a slight overweight to the region in portfolios given
earnings strength but favor an active approach.

MARKET VIEW
Another Tale of Market Rotation
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
October offered another reminder that headline index returns do not always tell the
full story. The S&P 500 ended the month slightly lower, down almost 1%, but the more
muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market leadership beneath the
surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the AI trade, particularly

across semiconductors and memory stocks, with the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index
experiencing a 19% intramonth drawdown. The dominant concerns in recent months
have centered on the sustainability of AI infrastructure spending and whether the scale
of investment will ultimately generate sufficient returns. Those only intensified amid a
sharp rise in bond yields after the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield spiked above 5.3% as of
October 5 for the first time since 2002, raising scrutiny over AI financing conditions and

whether higher borrowing costs could increase hurdle rates for continued investment
and slow the pace of capital spending.
Yet the pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S.
Equities. Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market,
with Value-oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. Value defeated
Growth in October, with the Russell 1000 Value Index outperforming its Growth

counterpart by 10 percentage points, an outcome seen in only about 1% of months since
1979. The move extends Value's outperformance for the year, with the index up 23%
year-to-date compared with down almost 1% for Growth. Participation also improved.
The equal-weighted S&P 500 outperformed its market-cap-weighted counterpart by its
widest monthly gap since 2009 (Exhibit 4A), while the share of S&P 500 constituents
trading above their 200-day moving average rebounded to 62% from just 43% at the

end of September, and 76% of stocks outperformed the index for the month.

The latest rotation is not the first attempt at a change in market leadership this year.
The more important question is what that shift represents: a fundamental reassessment
of the AI trade or another step toward broader, more mature market leadership?
Recent Q3 Technology earnings results have sharpened that debate. Investors are
increasingly looking beyond the scale of AI spending to companies’ ability to translate
that investment into durable growth and attractive returns. The result may be greater

dispersion not only between Growth and Value, but also among AI leaders as investors
become more discerning about which companies can justify the scale of their
investments.
Investment Implications
We continue to look for potential
missing opportunities and favor
diversification across and within asset
classes in a multi-asset portfolio.
In particular, we see potential
opportunities to broaden exposure
beyond AI leaders toward areas
where earnings, capital spending and

productivity trends are improving,
including Value, Small- and Mid-caps,
and select non-U.S. markets, particularly
EMs and Japan. As market leadership
evolves, maintaining diversified
exposures and periodically rebalancing
portfolios can help investors participate
in a broader set of possible return
drivers without becoming overly
dependent on any single theme.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
But greater selectivity does not necessarily signal the end of the AI investment cycle.
YoY growth rates may become harder to maintain after two years of rapid acceleration
as the comparison base rises, but absolute levels of AI capital spending remain elevated.
What matters more is whether spending translates into strong productivity and earnings
growth ahead and whether AI demand continues to support investment. October’s

volatility may therefore reflect less a retreat from the AI theme and more a recalibration
of valuations and expected returns, as other areas of the market begin to contribute
more meaningfully to market performance.
Several developments ahead will help determine whether that rotation has staying
power. One important test is whether broader participation can persist if pressure on AI
stocks continues. Continued strength outside the AI trade would support the case that

investors are reallocating capital rather than broadly reducing risk. The path of monetary
policy will also matter as investors assess where borrowing costs may be headed. The
Federal Reserve’s not-so-hawkish hold in October offered some reassurance, but the
path ahead remains dependent on the evolving balance between inflation and growth.
Any signs of resolution of geopolitical tensions remain an important swing factor
particularly as recent flashpoints in the Middle East have pressured energy prices higher

again. Results of the midterm elections should also provide greater visibility into the
policy landscape for the next few years. While the market does not need every clearing
event to resolve favorably, greater clarity around these uncertainties could help narrow
the range of potential outcomes facing investors.
For investors, October’s sudden change in leadership reinforces the importance of
diversification. Leadership can shift quickly, leaving portfolios that become overly

dependent on a single theme, sector or style particularly vulnerable when it does. We
remain constructive on U.S. Equities and believe that the market may be transitioning
more toward a mid-cycle phase in which returns become broader but also more
measured compared to the sharp acceleration earlier in the cycle. That argues not for
abandoning the market’s long-term winners, but for complementing them with exposure
to additional sources of returns across style, size, sector and region.

THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Reorienting around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction to Capital Goods
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
For all the questions (and recent market weakness) hanging over the AI trade, from
monetization and cash burn to financing risk and overcapacity, a measurable aspect is
the buildout itself. Data center construction is lifting construction spending and capital

goods orders, while companies are adding and reviving capacity to meet AI-related
demand. Still, planning delays, access to power, labor scarcity, local politics and the cost
of capital remain meaningful constraints on the buildout’s pace or profitability.
Corporate Plans. Take Caterpillar as one example that has resumed production of a
10-megawatt natural gas turbine discontinued back in 2022. A 175-year old glassmaker,
Corning, is leaning into optical connectivity as AI clusters require faster connections of

optical fiber cabling between servers. Caterpillar and Corning are just two companies
among others pivoting to supply data centers.
Construction to Capital Goods. The AI reorientation is evident in construction
spending as well, where data center construction has outpaced every other major
residential and nonresidential category. Over the last year AI-related construction on data
centers has surged more than 60% while spending on residential, lodging, commercial

or office has declined notably (Exhibit 5A). Data center construction is running at an $85
billion (B) annualized rate this year, versus roughly $49B a year earlier, and now accounts
for 11% of private nonresidential construction, up from just 2% in 2022 according to
the Census Bureau. Similarly, demand for artificial intelligence business equipment and
transportation equipment drove an increase in durable goods orders in September on the
back of new orders for U.S.-manufactured capital goods, suggesting business spending

on equipment remained robust in Q3. Another factor, see Exhibit 5B, where critical
components such as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers continue to
face long lead times that affect delivery timelines and ultimate profitability for new data
center development.
These signposts reinforce our view that we are only in the middle innings of the AI
investment cycle. From an investment perspective, huge demand for data centers, power
systems, networking infrastructure, specialized chips and other equipment continues

to strain supply, creating both bottlenecks and potential opportunities for companies
powering the buildout.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500/Market-cap Weighted Index is a market-capitalization-weighted index that is widely regarded as the best single gauge of large-cap U.S. equities. The index includes 500 leading

companies and covers approximately 80% of available market capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index includes the same constituents as the capitalization weighted S&P 500, but each company in the S&P 500 EWI is allocated a fixed weight - or 0.2% of the
index total at each quarterly rebalance.
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index is a broad-based, market-capitalization-weighted benchmark that measures the performance of the U.S. investment-grade, U.S. dollar-denominated,

fixed-rate taxable bond market
Bloomberg Commodity Index is a broadly diversified financial benchmark that tracks the price movements of physical commodities through futures contracts.
MSCI Emerging Market Index captures large and mid cap representation across 24 Emerging Markets (EM) countries.
United Nations Food and Agriculture Food Price Index is a measure of the monthly change in international prices of a basket of food commodities.
Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is a market-cap-weighted index focusing on 30 leading semiconductor firms, offering insights into the tech market.

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Document 297: Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-22)

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: risks mount
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:31 November 20 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s labour market disappointed again in October, employment falling 43.5k following
September’s 36.4k decline. On a three-month average basis, job gains over the year have now slowed
to 1.1%yr, materially below current growth in the working-age population, 1.7%yr. As a result, the

unemployment rate rose to a new post-pandemic high of 4.9% despite the participation rate falling
another 0.2ppts to 66.6%.

### Page 2

While employment growth’s descent initially reflected softer momentum in hiring, now we are seeing
evidence of a material increase in redundancies. Most affected workers look to have remained in
employment to date, but persistent job shedding raises the risk of a faster and more persistent uptrend
in the unemployment rate, potentially above our baseline peak forecast of 5.2% (at mid-2027) and most
certainly before the late-2028 peak of 5.1% the RBA expects.

As such, the robust 1.1% (3.5%yr) rise in the Q3 Wage Price Index warrants close scrutiny. The Q3
increase was skewed towards those on awards and enterprise arrangements – pay outcomes directly
tied to the Fair Work Commission’s decision and collective bargaining. Individual arrangements
meanwhile, the most responsive to current and expected labour market conditions, accounted for their
smallest share of a Q3 wage increase since 2019. Business survey detail also suggests labour cost

growth will normalise quickly after the award increase.
Deteriorating economic conditions are also increasingly evident in housing. The Westpac Housing
Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in November, among the weakest readings since 2005. Interestingly,
there remains considerable latent demand: almost 13% of consumers intend to purchase a home over
the next year, and relatively few plan to sell. For now, though, weak sentiment, high interest rates and

expectations of further price declines are keeping would-be buyers on the sidelines. These factors also
pose risks to the broader growth story.
In the US, abstracting from the Anthropic IPO, the outlook for inflation remained the critical theme. The
October FOMC minutes highlighted that inflation is currently being supported by tariffs and energy
prices as well as the AI buildout, with "several [members] judg[ing] that price pressures were
increasingly extending beyond industries directly affected by data center construction”. Participants

"assessed that medium- and longer-term inflation expectations continued to be consistent with the
Committee's 2 percent objective", although persistent above-target inflation is a threat to the status
quo, and perceived risks are skewed to the upside. FOMC members who spoke throughout the week
also kept the focus on price risks, and called for the FOMC to act swiftly. Governor Waller again
proposed a 50bp hike in December; others seemingly favour a 25bp hike at the December and January

meetings, as Westpac forecasts, and continuing to tighten further in 2027 if necessary.
Very clearly, the labour market and activity growth are not of concern for US policy makers. Retail sales
gave further justification for this view this week, the control group surprising to the upside with a 0.6%
gain in October after a material upward revision to September (0.5%). That said, housing is under
considerable pressure, pending home sales falling 17.1% over the past year, and housing starts back

at 2019 levels despite a 9.9% rebound in October. Persistent weakness in this sector will, in time, have
implications for both consumption and investment.
China’s October data round was the other key international development of the week. Again, it provided
a call for pro-active stimulus in scale. In the month, annual retail sales growth almost stalled at 0.4%yr
and the decline in fixed asset investment broadened outside property, with capital expenditure now

down 8.1% across the economy year-to-date. Industrial production again highlighted that its output to
satiate global demand which is holding up aggregate growth; though, even here, momentum softened
in October, annual growth in production slowing from 5.5%yr to 4.8%yr. It is not only activity that

### Page 3

Related articles
remains under pressure, but also wealth, with new / existing home prices 0.15% / 0.24% lower in the
month. As we continue to emphasise, to prove effective, stimulus needs to affect change in both activity
and wealth and do so in a sustainable manner.

The National Bank of Hungary kept rates unchanged in
November, but delivered a dovish twist. A rate cut was already
on the table, and improving domestic risk sentiment is creating
more room for manoeuvre. Nevertheless, we only see the next
cuts coming in late 2027 as inﬂation pressures rebuild

Peter Virovacz and Zoltán Homolya
The National Bank of Hungary, Budapest

ING's policy view: A dovish twist, but no
rush

### Page 2

The National Bank of Hungary kept the base rate unchanged at
5.50% on 17 November, in line with widespread expectations. This
marked the third consecutive meeting with rates being kept on
hold. While the latest communication revealed somewhat more
room for monetary easing, we think the window of opportunity will
remain narrow.
Looking ahead, the exceptionally favourable domestic inﬂation
backdrop is likely to fade relatively quickly. We see inﬂation

accelerating to 3.0% by December 2026, with repricing at the start
of next year temporarily pushing price pressures closer to 3.5% in
the ﬁrst quarter of 2027. Inﬂation could then approach 4% during
the summer, largely due to base eﬀects. In our view, this
increasingly challenging inﬂation proﬁle means that any near-term
opportunity for easing may prove short-lived.
In the meantime, however, we see the latest dovish shift in
communication potentially testing the waters and gauging market

sensitivity to eventual easing, particularly in a global environment
where hawkishness still dominates. The key litmus test will be the
forint: a muted FX reaction to the dovish rhetoric would give the
central bank greater conﬁdence that some easing could be
delivered without jeopardising market stability. This means that a
possible one-and-done December rate cut has become a clear
dovish risk to our baseline.
However, we believe that any near-term window for easing would

be relatively narrow. As 2027 progresses, the main constraint on
monetary policy is likely to shift gradually from external risks
towards the domestic inﬂation outlook. With inﬂation expected to
move closer to 4% during the summer, we see little room for a
sustained easing cycle over the next six to nine months. Any near-
term easing would therefore be more likely to represent an
opportunistic move than the beginning of a conventional cutting
cycle.

A more durable easing window could open later in 2027, once
inﬂation starts to decelerate more convincingly after the summer. If
this coincides with a benign external environment and a further
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.

Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

decline in the risk premium on Hungarian assets, we see room for
one or two rate cuts in the fourth quarter of 2027. Our baseline
therefore sees the base rate reaching 5.00% by end-2027, followed
by a further reduction to 4.50% by end-2028.
Our three takeaways from the presser

### Page 4

Review Policy rate NBH National Bank of Hungary
Monetary policy Hungary
1. A cut was already on the table. The biggest dovish surprise
came during the press conference. The Monetary Council
considered two options: keeping rates unchanged or cutting
them, while the decision itself was not unanimous. This
suggests that the bar for easing may already be lower than
previously thought. The December Inﬂation Report will now be
particularly important, with Governor Mihály Varga explicitly

stating that favourable developments in Hungary's risk
assessment and inﬂation outlook, together with reduced
external uncertainty, could increase its room for manoeuvre.
2. Hungary's risk story has improved. Perhaps the most notable
change in the narrative is the assessment of Hungary's risk
premium. Varga highlighted the favourable market reception of
the government's ﬁscal plans and its commitment to euro
adoption as factors supporting Hungary's risk assessment and

increasing room for monetary policy. The forint has also
remained stable and is stronger than a year ago, helping to
contain inﬂationary pressures. In other words, domestic
ﬁnancial market developments are becoming less of a
constraint on monetary policy than the Monetary Council
previously considered them to be.
3. The external environment still demands caution. The greater
room for manoeuvre does not mean that the NBH is ready to
embark on an easing cycle. Energy price volatility, elevated

global yields and above-target inﬂation in major economies
continue to warrant caution. At home, inﬂation is also expected
to accelerate from its current 2% level due to fuel prices, tax
changes and other policy measures. This leaves the Monetary
Council in a familiar data-dependent position: its room for
manoeuvre has increased, but whether it can actually use that
room will depend on both the external environment and the
December inﬂation outlook.

⽇上午（台北時間 21:52）以 8-K 公告。

治理結構同步調整：⾸度設⽴董事⻑，暫代執⾏⻑兼任財務⻑
⿈仁勳辭去總裁、執⾏⻑與董事，公司發⾔⼈表⽰其⽇後不以任何形式參與輝達。財務⻑ Colette Kress（2013 年加⼊，
曾任 Cisco 與 Microsoft 事業部財務主管）暫代執⾏⻑並續任財務⻑；1993 年起擔任董事、曾任 Synopsys 執⾏⻑之
Harvey C. Jones 出任輝達⾸任董事⻑（輝達過去不設董事⻑）。董事會將考慮內外部⼈選並委請外部公司協助，未公布時
程。第三季財報維持 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇公布；據眾議院外交委員會主席表⽰，⿈仁勳同意於感恩節前赴國會作證。

2027 年以前
2027 年之出貨⼤致鎖定：產品已量產、產能已預付、承諾已簽訂
Vera Rubin 已量產出貨，主要出貨量在 2027 年；Rubin Ultra 以前之規格已定案
輝達 8 ⽉表⽰ Vera Rubin 於當⽉開始量產出貨，管理層預估約占第三季資料中⼼營收兩成；鴻海表⽰ Vera Rubin 第四季
放量、為明年主⼒，廣達表⽰第四季⼩量、2027 年第⼀季放量。管理層 8 ⽉預估 2028 會計年度（約當 2027 年 2 ⽉⾄
2028 年 1 ⽉）營收成⻑約七成，屬供給受限之數字；3 ⽉ GTC 時稱 Blackwell 與 Vera Rubin ⾄ 2027 年之訂單能⾒度⾄少

1 兆美元。據供應鏈訪查，Rubin Ultra 與 Feynman 之開發已進⼊執⾏階段；新任管理層第⼀個能實質調整的，是
Feynman 世代之規格、代⼯與封裝分配。
供應商受合約保護：2,790 億美元之採購與產能承諾，加上客⼾之預付款
輝達 7 ⽉底之季報揭露表外之供應與產能採購承諾 2,790 億美元（前⼀季 1,190 億美元），主要為記憶體與製造設施，台積
電、HBM 廠與主要組裝廠 2027 年之產能多已被預訂。下游⽅⾯，廣達 11 ⽉ 11 ⽇法說會表⽰，迄今未⾒客⼾調整訂單，
客⼾若延後拉貨需負擔相關利息與費⽤；以 CoreWeave 為例，其積壓訂單約 1,325 億美元，未來三個⽉另有約 90 億美元
之客⼾預付款與押⾦，綁定的是機房與晶⽚。
近期之⼲擾來⾃轉換期與出⼝管制，⽽⾮需求
據供應鏈訪查，第四季 VR200、Trainium 3 與 TPU v8 之新平台轉換均因零組件與組裝良率⽽遞延，屬時程問題。較需留
意的是出⼝管制：據報美國商務部正敲定新規，把⾺來西亞、新加坡等 7 國納⼊較嚴格之限制，涵蓋 Blackwell 以上晶⽚

及遠端使⽤；和碩 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇法說會表⽰此類限制並⾮新規，已納⼊法務審核。國會亦把晶⽚追蹤（Chip Security Act）
與出⼝許可審查（AI Overwatch Act）列為國防授權法之候選條⽂。本次事件涉及外國情報與晶⽚流向之指控，調查結果
出爐前，管制收緊之機率已上升，台灣組裝廠出貨東南亞機房之合規成本將提⾼。
接班情境
三種接班路徑對產品節奏、供應商議價與客⼾融資之影響不同；ToAlpha 以內部技術主
管接任為基本情境
董事會之⾸要任務是穩定客⼾與⼯程團隊，內部⼈接任之機率最⾼
據報導與預測市場，內部⼈選以 CUDA 創始⼈、現負責⼤型雲端與 HPC 業務之 Ian Buck 呼聲最⾼，其次為暫代之
Kress；技術⻑ Michael Kagan、⾸席科學家 Bill Dally 與 GPU ⼯程主管 Jonah Alben 亦被提及。外部⼈選包括曾任職輝

### Page 4

達七年之 Arm 執⾏⻑ Rene Haas；Apple 硬體主管 Johny Srouji 據報亦在考慮之列，美國總統則據報公開推薦前 Apple
執⾏⻑ Tim Cook。全球業務主管 Jay Puri 已於 6 ⽉宣布退休。輝達沒有公開之接班計畫、約 60 位主管直接向⿈仁勳報
告；正式⼈選產⽣前由 Kress 暫代，市場預期約⼀年。ToAlpha 以內部技術主管接任為基本情境（45%），營運財務型（含
Kress 續任或外部營運型）30%，外部產業執⾏⻑ 25%。
無論何⼈接任，新增融資與規格升級之積極程度都可能低於⿈仁勳時代
輝達 7 ⽉底持有⾮上市股權約 512 億美元、上市股權約 428 億美元，另有最⾼ 1,085 億美元之保證與 250 億美元之股權投
資承諾；運算承諾⽅⾯，OpenAI 規劃約 12GW 之輝達運算、Anthropic 最多 1GW。已簽承諾會延續，但新增部分之審核
標準可能提⾼：據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最⾼ 1,050 億美元之擔保；市場亦期待新任管理層提⾼現⾦回饋、回應

「循環融資」之疑慮。規格升級⽅⾯，據供應鏈訪查，⿈仁勳之產品策略對供應鏈有「產能預訂」與「規格升級」兩項激
進影響，我們認為這兩項也是輝達供應鏈過去享有較⾼評價之重要原因。新任管理層若偏重成本與執⾏穩定，800V
HVDC、共同封裝光學、M9 等級銅箔基板等升級之導⼊可能放慢，供應鏈成本管理亦會更嚴格。

資料來源：ToAlpha。⼈選為媒體報導與預測市場所列，⾮公司公布；機率為 ToAlpha 主觀判斷，將隨董事會遴選進度調整。
產品路線
Feynman 以前之路線圖已進⼊執⾏，新任管理層可調整之處在決策速度與規格升級
路線圖本⾝未變，但 2026 年已出現取捨：Rubin CPX 擱置、Kyber 機櫃傳延⾄ 2028 年
Rubin 為兩顆光罩極限尺⼨晶粒、每顆搭載 288GB HBM4 之設計[5]，量產爬坡已受 HBM4 規格與良率牽制。輝達今年並
把原訂 2026 年底之 Rubin CPX 移出路線圖，改以 Groq 授權技術之 LPX 推論機櫃處理解碼；產業研究機構 7 ⽉指出
Kyber 機櫃因 PCB 中背板之可製造性延⾄ 2028 年，輝達回應路線圖不變。這顯⽰即使在⿈仁勳任內，年更節奏已觸及供

應鏈之物理極限。我們認為新任管理層較可能之調整，是把年更改為「⼤世代兩年、中間做強化版」，並集中資源於推論
（LPX、Vera CPU）與網路（Spectrum-X、共同封裝光學）。
開放策略可能加速：NVLink Fusion 讓 ASIC 客⼾也採購輝達元件
NVLink Fusion 已納⼊ AWS Trainium4、Marvell 客製 XPU 與 Intel 客製 x86 CPU，輝達 3 ⽉並⼊股 Marvell 20 億美元。
對新任管理層⽽⾔，與其與 ASIC 正⾯競爭，不如在 ASIC 機櫃內銷售 CPU、網卡、交換器與 NVLink；輝達之營收來源因
此分散，但 GPU 本⾝之份額讓渡也會更快。對台灣供應鏈⽽⾔，交換器、網卡相關供應商不必選邊。

資料來源：輝達、鴻海、廣達、和碩、產業研究機構、據供應鏈訪查；ToAlpha 整理。
AMD∕博通∕ASIC
Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受益，CoWoS 份額之移轉在事件前已開始
事件前之路徑：輝達占 CoWoS 需求由 2026 年 56% 降⾄ 2027 年 45%
據供應鏈訪查，2027 年全球 CoWoS 需求約 2,694 千⽚，輝達 1,222 千⽚、成⻑ 57%，⾮輝達合計成⻑ 140%；AMD 因
OpenAI、Meta 與 Anthropic 之 MI450 系列放量，份額由 9% 升⾄ 20%，聯發科（Google TPU）由 3% 升⾄ 7%。產業研
究機構亦預期 TPU 出貨 2027 年倍增以上，⾼於輝達⾼階 GPU 約三成之成⻑。換⾔之，輝達之份額下滑早於醜聞；接班不

確定性讓既有趨勢加速，⽽⾮創造新趨勢。
Google TPU 最直接：客⼾與供應鏈之信任轉移最快
據供應鏈訪查，在輝達⾯臨接班不確定性下，Google TPU 最可能先取得客⼾與供應鏈之進⼀步信任，設計夥伴博通與聯發
科最直接受惠。博通 9 ⽉表⽰ AI 營收 2026 會計年度約 580 億美元、2027 年約 1,150 億、2028 年約 2,300 億美元，六家
XPU 客⼾中 Anthropic 2027 年部署 5GW、2028 年再 10GW，OpenAI ⾃研推論晶⽚ 2027 年 1.3GW、2028 年逾 5GW。聯
發科表⽰第⼆顆 ASIC 按計畫於 2028 年量產。
AMD：具名承諾最多 14GW，2027 年起才⼤量部署，能否接住移轉取決於 Helios 機櫃之量產速度
AMD 第三季資料中⼼營收 85.5 億美元、年增 97%，MI455X 已開始量產出貨，具名承諾 OpenAI 6GW、Meta 6GW、
Anthropic 2GW。據供應鏈訪查，Helios 機櫃之⼤量出貨在 2027 年第⼆季。AMD 之 MI350 把 3 奈⽶運算晶粒 3D 堆疊於

6 奈⽶ I/O 晶粒之上[4]，MI450 系列據報再採 2 奈⽶運算晶粒、每顆 432GB HBM4，以單晶⽚規格⾒⻑，但機櫃級整合之
經驗仍少於輝達。輝達若在 2027 年邊際讓出份額，AMD 之瓶頸是 CoWoS 與 HBM 配額，⽽⾮客⼾意願。
⾦額份額與顆數份額差距⼤：ASIC 顆數 2028 年超越 GPU，⾦額仍以輝達為主
產業研究機構估計客製 ASIC 出貨顆數將於 2028 年超越資料中⼼ GPU；以⾦額計，據供應鏈訪查 ASIC 占加速器市場由約
⼀成升⾄ 2027 年約兩成。輝達 Vera Rubin 每 GW 營收約 400 億美元，博通估其每 GW 內容價值約 250 億美元（其估計之
中值）。份額移轉對輝達營收之影響因此⼩於對出貨顆數之影響；但對依顆數與機櫃數計價之台灣組裝與零組件供應商，影
響較接近顆數。
技術差距縮⼩：AMD 與 ASIC 之單晶⽚規格追近，輝達之優勢轉向機櫃整合與軟體
以廠商公布之稠密峰值計，AMD MI455X 之 FP4 約 40 PFLOPS、Rubin 約 35 PFLOPS[2]（輝達官⽅標⽰之 50 PFLOPS 為

NVFP4 推論值）；Google TPU 歷經五代維持架構穩定，以規模與能效取勝[1]。輝達之差異化愈來愈落在 NVLink 機櫃整
合、網路與 CUDA ⽣態，⽽這些正是需要領導⼈跨部⾨拍板之領域；接班空窗期之⾵險，在於競爭者單晶⽚規格追近之
際，輝達之系統整合節奏放慢。

### Page 6

ToAlpha 份額移轉之推導：融資收斂與客⼾分散下單，2027 年約 2、2028 年約 4 個百分點
2027 年：明年約四分之⼀業務需輝達融資⽀撐，若新增融資縮減⼀⾄⼆成，約為輝達量 2.5%〜5%，以 45% 份額換算約
1〜2 個百分點，加上客⼾邊際分散下單，取 2 個百分點（1.5〜2.5）。2028 年：融資依賴之需求縮減約兩成（約輝達量
5%），加上⼤型客⼾新專案約 4% 轉向 AMD 與 ASIC，合計約輝達量 9%、約 4 個百分點（3〜5）。輝達減少之量，2027 年
七成、2028 年⼋成由 AMD 與 ASIC 依原份額吸收，其餘為依賴輝達融資、隨之消失之需求，CoWoS 總量因此少約 0.6%、
0.8%。供應商營收以平台出貨量⽐例估算，未區分每⽚晶圓之價值差異。

之⽐重為 ToAlpha 依法說會說法取整之假設，個別公司可能與組別平均差異明顯；台積電與⽇⽉光投控以 CoWoS 總量計。影響為 AI 營收，⾮淨利。
韓國∕中國∕美國
台灣份額 2027 年前難以外移；韓國之⾓⾊在⿈仁勳任內已擴⼤，美國是⽣產地點之轉
移，中國沒有承接能⼒
物理限制：輝達之 CoWoS 約九成在台灣封裝，美國之 CoWoS 級產能最早 2028 年初
據供應鏈訪查，輝達 2027 年約 1,222 千⽚之 CoWoS 需求中，約 132 千⽚由 Amkor 承接，其餘由台積電封裝。美國⽅

⾯，Amkor 亞利桑那廠 2028 年初量產、台積電亞利桑那封裝廠尚無 CoWoS 量產時程、SK 海⼒⼠印第安那 HBM 封裝廠
6 / 12

### Page 7

2029 年、三星泰勒⼆廠 2030 年；亞利桑那⽣產之 Blackwell 晶圓⽬前仍須運回台灣封裝。因此 2027 年以前之 Rubin 與
Rubin Ultra，前段晶圓與封裝⾼度依賴台灣；新任管理層第⼀個可調整之世代是 Feynman。
韓國：HBM 主要在韓國，增量在封裝與代⼯，且多為⿈仁勳任內之決定
據業界估計，Rubin 之 HBM4 由 SK 海⼒⼠供應約三分之⼆、三星約四分之⼀以上；產業研究機構 6 ⽉⼤幅上修三星之
HBM4 份額，三星今年亦發表單堆疊 36GB、3.3TB/s 之 HBM4[3]。台灣在 HBM 之⾓⾊主要為美光在台產能。韓國之增量
有兩處：推論晶⽚ LP30 交由三星 4 奈⽶；據報輝達 10 ⽉宣布與 Amkor ⾄ 2028 年底把韓國中介層產能提⾼ 300% 以上。
另據報輝達要求記憶體廠把客製 HBM 之 base die 交台積電代⼯，則對台灣有利。新任管理層若偏重成本與供應分散，韓
國在⾮旗艦產品之代⼯與封裝份額可能再提⾼，但旗艦 GPU 之前段 2028 年前仍無替代。

中國：管制只會收緊，台廠之⾵險在出貨中國客⼾與東南亞機房
輝達財測沿⽤不計⼊中國資料中⼼運算營收之做法；產業研究機構估計 2026 年上半年輝達在中國 AI 加速器之份額降⾄
34%、華為升⾄ 28%。本次事件涉及晶⽚流向中國之指控，新任管理層之合規只會更嚴，供應鏈不會流向中國；受影響的
是出貨中國客⼾或東南亞機房之台灣組裝廠，例如英業達 5 ⽉法說會表⽰其 AI 伺服器以中國客⼾占⽐偏⾼。
美國：地點轉移由台廠執⾏，營收仍在台灣公司，但成本較⾼
美國商務部⻑表⽰⽬標把台灣約四成半導體產能移⾄美國；輝達宣⽰在美國⽣產最多 5,000 億美元之 AI 基礎設施，緯創德
州廠已⽣產 GB300 並準備 Vera Rubin，鴻海休⼠頓廠興建中、規劃⽣產 GB300 運算模組。新任執⾏⻑在國安疑慮之政治
環境下，加速「美國製造」之誘因更⾼；但承接者仍是台積電、鴻海、緯創、廣達等台廠，屬地點轉移。美國本⼟之替代
以 Intel 與 Amkor 為主：據報 Feynman 之 I/O 晶粒與最多四分之⼀之最終封裝可能交 Intel，據供應鏈訪查 Google TPU

後續世代預計採 Intel EMIB-T 封裝；這類分散化之決定權在新任管理層⼿上。

件
台廠為主 美國廠多在興建 規格升級節奏放慢之影響⼤於份額
資料來源：輝達、台積電、Amkor、SK 海⼒⼠、三星、產業研究機構、據供應鏈訪查；ToAlpha 整理。
直接供應鏈
量之衝擊⼩於規格升級節奏之衝擊：2027 年之價值成⻑相當部分來⾃ Vera Rubin 之規
格
機櫃數之估計分歧，單櫃價值翻倍是共同看法
據供應鏈訪查，NVL72 機櫃出貨 2026 年約 82 千櫃、2027 年約 90 千櫃；產業研究機構則估計 2027 年成⻑逾五成，兩者
差距⼤。共同之看法是 Vera Rubin 系統平均售價約為 GB300 之兩倍，廣達亦表⽰新世代 GPU 伺服器單價⾼四到五成。
7 / 12

### Page 8

2027 年輝達供應鏈之成⻑相當部分來⾃單櫃價值，⽽單櫃價值來⾃規格升級，這正是新任管理層最可能放慢之部分。組裝
份額⽅⾯，鴻海由 2026 年約 37% 升⾄ 2027 年約 43%，廣達約 26%，緯創約 24%。
受影響之先後：規格升級受惠者最先，依機櫃數計價者次之，晶圓與封裝最後
800V HVDC 電源（台達電、光寶科）、共同封裝光學、M9 等級銅箔基板（台光電、聯茂）、液冷與雙⾯冷板（奇鋐、雙鴻）
等受惠於規格升級之供應商，其時點已隨 Kyber 傳延⾄ 2028 年；更替使「升級是否如期」之不確定性增加。依機櫃數計
價之組裝、滑軌、機構件次之。台積電與 CoWoS 因產能不⾜、可轉給 AMD 與 ASIC，受影響最⼩。另據供應鏈訪查，台灣
供應商將⾯臨更嚴格之價格談判，訂單亦可能進⼀步分散；新任管理層若以成本管理⾒⻑，台灣組裝廠本已偏薄之⽑利率
將承壓。
終端客⼾結構亦重要：neocloud 對輝達融資之依賴⾼於⼤型雲端業者
⼤型雲端業者以⾃有現⾦流與⻑約採購，neocloud 與前沿實驗室則較依賴輝達之⼊股、保底合約與擔保。若新任管理層收

緊新增融資，受影響之需求集中在 neocloud；和碩表⽰其伺服器⽬前以 neocloud 與⽩牌為主。同樣以輝達平台為主，客
⼾以⼤型雲端業者為主者（如廣達）較為穩定。
台股曝險
以輝達平台為主之台股：2027 年影響有限，不利處在缺少 ASIC 動能與 2028 年之份額
移轉
市場⽬前只區分出 ASIC：以 ASIC 為主者相對抗跌，純輝達與兩邊都有者差異不⼤
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇⾄ 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇，加權指數 −7.1%，32 家中以 ASIC 為主之 10 家平均 −9.4%、以輝達平台為主之 5 家 −14.0%、
兩邊都有之 17 家 −17.0%；11 ⽉ 12 ⽇傳出董事會準備撤換之當⽇，三組分別 −5.2%、−5.3%、−2.8%（加權 −2.6%）。此
期間同時有美國期中選舉後之科技股修正，兩邊都有之組別多為前波漲幅⼤之零組件股，跌幅亦⼤，個股差異不全來⾃本
事件。市場對純輝達者尚未額外折價，反映 2027 年之訂單能⾒度。
純輝達之台股：2027 年營收有保護，不利處在缺少 ASIC 動能與 2028 年之份額移轉

以輝達平台為主之五家，2027 年之 Vera Rubin 出貨已排定，單價提⾼四到五成，營收成⻑仍可期；ToAlpha 基本情境之
份額移轉僅使其 2027 年 AI 營收相對原本路徑少約 3.4%。不利之處有三：其⼀，缺少 2027〜2028 年成⻑最快之 ASIC 動
能；其⼆，2028 年份額移轉使其 AI 營收少約 6.7%，若規格升級放慢，單櫃價值之成⻑亦打折；其三，依賴輝達之配貨與
融資，新任管理層之成本與融資政策直接影響其⽑利與客⼾。廣達已表⽰⾮ GPU 平台 2027 年將升⾄雙位數、和碩有 4〜5
家 ASIC 客⼾將於 2027 年放量，組裝廠可逐步降低集中度；我們認為華碩、技嘉之伺服器業務較難轉向 ASIC，因雲端業者
⾃研晶⽚多直接委託 ODM。華碩、技嘉、和碩之⾮ AI 業務占⽐亦⾼，股價受 AI 以外因素影響⼤。緯創⺟公司以輝達運算
板為主，惟合併⼦公司緯穎後屬兩邊都有。
兩邊都有之供應商：份額移轉之影響約 −1%，⾵險在規格升級節奏
奇鋐表⽰「GPU 與 ASIC ⾮⼆選⼀、兩邊都出貨」；據供應鏈訪查，雙鴻之 ASIC 營收 2027 年可望約占四成。這類供應商在

份額移轉下之 AI 營收相對原本路徑少約 1.3%（2027 年）、2.3%（2028 年），其⾵險在規格升級之節奏，⽽⾮平台選擇。
台積電與⽇⽉光投控依 CoWoS 總量計，影響約 −0.6%、−0.8%。

觀察指標
未來三個⽉之五個觀察點，決定份額移轉落在情境區間之哪⼀端
11 ⽉ 17 ⽇第三季財報：暫代執⾏⻑是否重申 2028 會計年度約七成之成⻑、Vera Rubin 之放量節奏，以及回購與新增
融資之態度。
正式執⾏⻑⼈選：內部技術主管、營運財務型或外部產業執⾏⻑，對應圖表 3 之三種情境。
供應鏈之產能預訂與規格定案：台積電 2027 年 CoWoS 分配、HBM4E 與 Rubin Ultra 之規格、800V HVDC 與共同封裝

光學之量產時點有無延後。
出⼝管制：⿈仁勳赴國會作證後，晶⽚追蹤與出⼝審查法案能否併⼊國防授權法、東南亞 7 國限制是否成案，以及對伺
服器出貨之實際影響。
客⼾之分散下單：⼤型雲端業者與 neocloud 是否把邊際訂單轉向 AMD 與 ASIC，以及 AMD Helios 之量產速度。
⾵險
本報告之主要不確定性在於接班⼈選、調查結果與 CoWoS 產能是否仍不⾜
接班⼈選與調查結果：董事會調查與國會調查若發現更多出⼝管制問題，影響可能擴及輝達之營運許可，份額移轉將超
出本報告區間；反之若迅速確定具技術背景之接班⼈，2028 年之移轉可能⼩於基本情境。
產能與吸收假設：本報告假設 CoWoS 與 HBM 2027〜2028 年仍不⾜、輝達減少之量七⾄⼋成由 AMD 與 ASIC 吸收。若
產能轉為過剩或依賴融資之需求消失更多，份額移轉將伴隨總量下降，對台積電與兩邊都有之供應商亦不利。
分組與⽐重：各公司之輝達⽐重為依法說會說法之 ToAlpha 判斷，部分公司未揭露客⼾結構；供應商營收以平台出貨量

⽐例估算，未區分每⽚晶圓之價值。個別公司之實際影響可能與所屬分組平均有明顯差異。
股價區間：事件期間之股價差異同時受美國期中選舉後之科技股修正與個別公司消息影響，不宜全數歸因於本事件。

本報告由 ToAlpha（toalpha.tw）研究部編製，屬純產業研究，⾮投資建議。本報告僅描述產業、供應鏈與情境影響，不對任何個股提供評等、⽬標
價或買進、賣出、持有之建議，亦不構成任何證券之要約或要約之引誘。報告中之份額變動、營收影響與情境機率係 ToAlpha 依公開資料、供應鏈訪
查與⾃訂假設推算，涉及多項假設，實際結果可能與本報告所述有重⼤差異。本報告所引⽤之資料雖來⾃我們認為可靠之來源，惟 ToAlpha 不保證其

Document 410: US SECTORS CALL_ Financials, Health Care & Consumer Staples

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US SECTORS CALL: Financials, Health Care & Consumer Staples
Date:    5 hours ago
US SECTORS CALL: Financials, Health Care & Consumer Staples

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL: Financials,
Health Care & Consumer
Staples
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 15 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Stocks bounced back last week after the prior week’s AI selloff. The S&P
500 rose 2.5% and is up 10.5% ytd. The chip trade rebounded, but
leadership changed hands. Nvidia’s board opened an investigation into
allegations against CEO Jensen Huang, who resigned on Thursday. CFO
Colette Kress is interim CEO. Investors moved into the stocks of Nvidia’s
rivals. AMD rose 19% for the week, and Broadcom rose 18% as Nvidia fell
13%.
Health Care and Financials have outperformed since the market’s March

30 low. Health Care is up 31.1% and Financials 21.1%, both ahead of the
S&P 500’s 19.2% (chart). Staples has lagged, up just 12.3% over the
same period, even though it's up 20.2% ytd.
Here’s more on Financials, Health Care, and Consumer Staples:
(1) Financials. The IPO window is wide open. SpaceX raised $75 billion
in June, the largest IPO ever, and its shares rose 19% on the first day.
Anthropic’s shares are expected to begin trading on Thursday at $70-$78

each, implying a fully diluted valuation of $1.26 trillion to $1.40 trillion. Its
S-1 filing affirmed that AI customers will pay up for compute. In Q2,
Anthropic earned $2.08 of revenue per dollar of compute and
infrastructure expense, up fivefold from $0.40 in Q1-2025 (in Q3, however,
the figure dipped to $1.72, as the company bought costlier short-term
capacity to meet demand). Big AI listings bring fees for underwriters,
trading desks, and the asset managers who buy the deals (chart).

### Page 3

Analysts have expected a strong year for capital markets. They
collectively forecast Investment Banking & Brokerage revenues to grow
21.1% this year, up from 7.4% in December (chart). Earnings are forecast
to grow 30.9%. Yet the industry’s forward P/E has fallen to 13.6 from 19.1
earlier this year. Investors are not paying for the deal boom.
The Financials sector’s forward profit margin is a record 22.1%, and the
profitability is broad-based. Forward profit margins are at or near record

highs across the sector’s biggest industries: Asset Management &
Custody Banks (28.6%), Regional Banks (28.5%), Investment Banking
(28.1%), and Diversified Banks (27.8%) (chart).

### Page 4

The Financials sector’s forward P/E has fallen to 15.2 from 16.4 at year-
end 2025, while its forward earnings is up 14.0% ytd. The sector has been
getting cheaper as it gets more profitable. We retain our overweight rating
on Financials.
(2) Health Care. Health Care has been the best-performing sector since
March 30. It is up 21.8% ytd. Forward earnings is up 11.5% ytd, and the
forward P/E is up 9.4% (chart). It trades at 19.8 times forward earnings, a

premium to the S&P 500’s 18.2.
Health Care’s profitability has been turning up. The sector’s forward profit
margin has risen to 8.7% from a low of 7.9% in January (chart). Within the
sector, the Biotechnology industry’s forward profit margin is at 33.4% and
Pharmaceuticals’ is at 31.1%; both have been climbing.

### Page 5

The Pharmaceuticals industry accounts for 37% of the sector’s market
capitalization. Analysts collectively expect Pharma earnings to fall 5.9%
this year, then jump 29.6% in 2027 (chart). But they’ve more than doubled
their revenue sights this year: The consensus 2026 revenue estimate
implies growth of 9.9%, up from 4.0% in December. The rebound rests on
sales, not just a low base from which the growth is measured.
AI should speed progress up in the medical world. In Anthropic’s IPO

roadshow video on Tuesday, CEO Dario Amodei claimed that AI can
compress 50 to 100 years of medical research into five to ten. We think
the benefits to drug discovery could be huge.
Investors already see it. Moderna is up 623.8% ytd following the success
of its personalized cancer vaccine, designed with the help of machine

### Page 6

learning. Clinical trials and approvals still take years, so the payoff will
build over time. Pharma’s 2027 rebound doesn’t depend on it. We
continue to recommend overweighting Health Care.
(3) Consumer Staples. Staples is one of only three sectors that got more
expensive this year. Its forward P/E has risen to 23.9 from 20.8 at the end
of 2025. Over the same period, the S&P 500’s P/E fell to 18.2 from 22.2,
and IT’s fell to 17.2 from 26.5 (chart). Only Real Estate and Consumer

Discretionary trade at higher multiples.
Forward earnings is up just 4.6% ytd, while the forward P/E is up 13.5%
(chart). Almost all of that re-rating came after late September, when
investors fled the AI trade for defensive stocks.

### Page 7

Profitability has not improved. The sector’s forward profit margin is 7.1%,
below its 2021 peak of 7.7% (chart). Analysts have cut their 2027 earnings
growth estimate to 5.6% from 7.3% in August. Investors are paying 23.9
times forward earnings for single-digit-percentage growth.
Walmart and Target are set to report quarterly earnings next week. The
Merchandise Retail industry trades at 36.4 times forward earnings, its
highest forward P/E since earlier this year (chart). Investors pay up for

Consumer Staples stocks when they fear an economic slowdown. But the
labor market is picking up, and consumers are spending. That makes the
valuation premium characteristic of defensive sectors hard to justify. We
retain our underweight rating on Consumer Staples.

### Page 8

clause.

事件  1
11/17 輝達第三季營收 1,158 億美元、年增 103% ，第四季指引 1,300 億美元
五⼤重點
1   輝達 11/17 公布第三季（截⾄ 10/25 ）營收 1,158 億美元，季增 20% 、年增 103% ，⾼於市場預期
約 1,088 億美元；資料中⼼營收 1,076 億美元，⾮ GAAP （⾮⼀般公認會計原則）每股盈餘 2.68 美

元。
2   第四季營收指引 1,300 億美元（正負 3% ，含多⼀週），⽑利率指引由第三季 74.9% 降⾄ 71% ⾄
72% ；公司指引未計⼊任何中國資料中⼼運算營收，第三季對中國 Hopper 出貨為 24 億美元。

### Page 2

3   財務⻑說明，第三季⽑利率⼩降反映 Vera Rubin 初期放量；存貨由 316 億升⾄ 397 億美元、應收
帳款天數由 60 天升⾄ 64 天，公司歸因於 Rubin 出貨節奏與部分投資級⼤客戶延⻑付款條件。
4   輝達正常盤收跌 0.44% ⾄ 155.46 美元，盤後漲約 3.8% ⾄ 161.35 美元；暫代執⾏⻑克雷斯稱
Rubin 「爆發性起步」，董事會主席表⽰無意讓⿈仁勳回任，將考慮內外部⼈選。
5   供應與產能承諾由 2,790 億增⾄ 3,900 億美元，主要為多年期記憶體採購，未來承諾總額 5,180
億美元；同⽇放空機構渾⽔（ Muddy Waters ）宣布放空輝達，治理疑慮未除。
三⼤結論
1   財報確認既有 AI 需求仍在加速交付，營收超預期與 Rubin 量產回應了換帥後對產品節奏的疑
慮；晶圓代⼯、先進封裝與記憶體的訂單能⾒度獲⽀撐，但⽑利指引下滑代表新平台爬坡成本由
輝達先吸收。
2   存貨、應收天數與採購承諾同步上升，且部分⼤客戶付款條件延⻑，顯⽰營收擴張伴隨營運資

⾦占⽤增加；這與⾼槓桿新雲端依賴外部融資的⾵險相連，供應鏈評價仍需以回款與現⾦流驗
證。
3   當輝達後續季度應收天數回落、存貨隨 Rubin 出貨下降，且台積電營收維持指引時，應分批維
持晶圓代⼯、先進封裝與記憶體核⼼部位；若新管理層縮減對新雲端的擔保或回款再延⻑，應先
降低 ODM 與⾼波動雲端衛星。
事件  2
11/17 紐約 Fed 總裁威廉斯稱通膨頑固，仍需升息但無須⼤動作
五⼤重點
1   紐約 Fed 總裁威廉斯 11/17 在紐約 Axios 論壇表⽰，通膨「頑固偏⾼」，關稅與能源衝擊正擴散⾄
更廣泛的通膨數據， Fed 要讓通膨回到 2% 仍有更多⼯作要做。
2   他認為經濟強勁且持續轉強、勞動市場穩健，「需求明顯跑贏供給」，但當前情勢不需要採取激
進的政策⾏動；⽬前聯邦資⾦利率⽬標區間為 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% 。
3   威廉斯為 FOMC （聯邦公開市場委員會）常任票委，談話後利率期貨隱含 12 ⽉升息⼀碼機率約
96% 、升息兩碼約 4% ，⼤幅升息的可能幾乎被排除。

4   他同時指出 AI 產業融資「變得更不透明且複雜」，但⽬前未看到 AI 帶來⾦融穩定⾵險；美國 10
年期公債殖利率 11/17 收約 5.10% ，仍處 5% 上⽅。

### Page 3

5   同⽇聖路易 Fed 總裁穆薩萊姆表⽰，實際政策利率需要再上升約 1 個百分點，貨幣政策必須⽴即
反應，鷹派官員主張的緊縮幅度明顯⼤於威廉斯。
三⼤結論
1   常任票委確認 12 ⽉升息⼀碼是基準、但排除兩碼，政策討論由「是否升息」轉為「還要升幾
次」；短端利率將停留⾼檔，⻑天期成⻑股的折現率壓⼒延續。
2   官員對緊縮幅度分歧，核⼼服務與能源擴散的數據將決定終點利率；短天期國庫券兼具收益與
再配置彈性，⻑天期公債在通膨與供給壓⼒未改善前仍應有限度配置。
3   當 11/19 公布的會議紀要與後續核⼼通膨顯⽰多數委員⽀持連續升息時，應維持短券核⼼並延
後增加⻑債久期；若核⼼服務⽉增回落且公債標售需求改善，才逐步延⻑存續期間。
事件  3
11/17 美國 10 ⽉零售銷售⽉增 0.2% ，⼯業⽣產⽉減 0.4%
五⼤重點
1   美國商務部普查局公布 10 ⽉零售銷售初值 7,482 億美元，⽉增 0.2% ，符合預期、低於 9 ⽉修正
後的 0.8% ；排除汽⾞及零件⽉增 0.6% ，整體年增 6.8% 。

2  Fed 公布 10 ⽉⼯業⽣產⽉減 0.4% ，遜於預期的⽉增 0.1% ，主因公⽤事業產出⽉減 4.3% 、礦業
減 0.8% ；製造業產出反⽽⽉增 0.3% ，產能利⽤率由 76.8% 降⾄ 76.4% 。
3   普查局說明，零售⽉增 0.2% 的信賴區間為正負 0.4% ，統計上無法確認為正成⻑；排除汽⾞後
⽉增 0.6% ⾼於整體，代表汽⾞及零件銷售下滑（推算）。
4   勞⼯統計局同⽇公布 10 ⽉進⼝物價⽉增 1.1% ，⾼於預期的 0.7% ，進⼝成本持續上升，與威廉
斯所說關稅與能源衝擊正向更廣泛物價擴散的判斷⼀致。
5   零售年增 6.8% 含物價上漲因素；商務部先前公布 9 ⽉個⼈儲蓄率已降⾄ 3.8% ，家庭以降低儲蓄
維持⽀出，⾯對油價或就業衝擊的緩衝變薄。
三⼤結論
1  10 ⽉消費放緩但未轉弱，⼯業⽣產下滑主要來⾃公⽤事業⽽⾮製造業，整體仍符合景氣擴
張；需求韌性加上進⼝物價⾛⾼，⽀持 Fed 在 12 ⽉續升息的基準。
2   名⽬零售成⻑含價格因素、家庭儲蓄緩衝下降，消費類股應區分客群收⼊穩定的企業與依賴信

⽤擴張的商品零售，不宜以名⽬銷售成⻑直接推估利潤。

### Page 4

3   當 11 ⽉零售、實質消費與初領失業⾦⼈數同時轉弱時，應提⾼現⾦與⾼品質債券⽐重；若消
費維持⽽核⼼通膨仍⾼，股票與⻑債可能同步承壓，應維持短券與現⾦流較強的股票。
事件  4
11/17 美軍空襲伊朗格什姆島⾶彈基地，布蘭特原油收 93.14 美元
五⼤重點
1   美國軍⽅官員表⽰，美軍戰機 11/17 以精準彈藥空襲伊朗⾰命衛隊位於荷莫茲海峽格什姆島的
⾶彈發射場，稱是回應伊朗針對美⽅⼈員與設施的攻擊，屬⾃衛⾏動。
2   伊朗地⽅官員稱攻擊未造成住宅與基礎設施損害；⾰命衛隊表⽰以無⼈機攻擊巴林伊薩空軍基
地的美軍雷達，海軍並稱第四艘油輪在荷莫茲海峽東部觸雷。
3   布蘭特原油 11/17 收 93.14 美元、漲 0.76% ，⻄德州原油收 86.01 美元、漲 0.34% ；盤中卡達外交
部稱美伊調解仍在持續，油價⼀度轉跌，漲幅受限。
4   伊朗總統裴澤斯基安表⽰，伊朗正以外交途徑尋求結束「不戰不和」局⾯，外⻑並已向阿曼蘇
丹通報結束戰爭的倡議，軍事衝突與和談訊號同時存在。

5   美軍先前稱已清除荷莫茲主要航道⽔雷，第四艘油輪觸雷顯⽰航道⾵險未消除；該海峽平時承
載全球約五分之⼀⽯油運量，美國仍對伊朗港⼝實施海上封鎖。
三⼤結論
1   美⽅由 11/16 暫緩打擊轉為 11/17 有限度空襲，地緣溢價重回油價，但調解仍在進⾏、布蘭特僅
⼩漲，市場仍把衝突定價為有限升級⽽⾮全⾯斷供。
2   油價停在 90 美元附近，能源通膨向核⼼物價擴散的⾵險仍在，與 Fed 官員對能源衝擊的擔憂⼀
致；原油與能源股宜維持⼩型事件部位，不以單⼀軍事新聞擴⼤配置。
3   當荷莫茲觸雷與攻擊擴⼤、布蘭特重回 100 美元以上時，應提⾼能源衛星作為通膨避險；若美
伊和談取得進展且供給修復，應降低原油部位並留意上游獲利下修。

11/17 台股再跌 947 點收 40,092 點，外資連 2 ⽇賣超
五⼤重點

### Page 5

1   台灣加權指數 11/17 收 40,092.11 點，下跌 947.97 點、跌幅 2.31% ，盤中最低 39,786.45 點；櫃買
指數跌 3.92% ，上市櫃下跌 1,767 家、上漲僅 454 家。
2   台積電收 2,170 元、跌 2.03% ；上市三⼤法⼈合計賣超 547.2 億元，其中外資賣超 380.9 億元、
⾃營商賣超 157.0 億元，外資近 20 ⽇累計賣超約 1.14 兆元。
3   ⾼盛 11/17 將台股評等由「加碼」降⾄「中⽴」，加權指數⽬標由 57,500 點降⾄ 50,000 點；台股
早盤⼀度跌 3.1% 後短暫翻紅，終場仍收低。
4   兩⽇累計下跌約 3,583 點、跌幅約 8.2% （推算）；賣壓延續輝達換帥後的治理疑慮與美國遠端
算⼒管制，美股晶⽚股在輝達財報前同樣遭到賣壓。
5   上市融資餘額近 20 ⽇減少約 83 萬張，仍在近⼀年中段；台股市值加權本益⽐約 18.9 倍、預估本
益⽐ 17.5 倍，評價已隨指數回落，但外資賣壓尚未停歇。

三⼤結論
1   兩⽇重挫主要反映 AI ⿓頭治理與出⼝管制帶來的評價修正；輝達隨後公布的營收與 Rubin 出貨
優於預期，台灣上游交付基本⾯未⾒轉弱，股價與基本⾯的落差擴⼤。
2   外資持續賣超、企業保留美元與美台利差擴⼤，台幣承壓格局未變；台股⾼評分與台幣低評分
可同時成⽴，持有台股的本幣報酬須另計匯率影響。
3   當 11/20 公布的 10 ⽉外銷訂單延續強勁、外資賣超收斂時，應分批維持晶圓代⼯與先進封裝核
⼼部位；若融資再擴張或客戶回款轉弱，應先降低 ODM 與⾼波動中游衛星。

11/17 Anthropic 獲准在那斯達克掛牌，維持每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元發⾏區間
五⼤重點
1  Anthropic 11/17 向美國證管會提交修正版 S-1 ，發⾏規模與條件不變，每股價格區間維持 70 ⾄
78 美元，並獲准以代號 ANT 在那斯達克全球精選市場掛牌，預計 11/19 開始交易。
2   本次發⾏ 8.0135 億股新股，創辦⼈另出售 2,365 萬股；以區間中值 74 美元計，新股募資約 593

億美元（推算），另有 260 億美元⾮投票權 N 類股私募同步進⾏。
3   發⾏完成後亞⾺遜將持有 14.7% 流通股本、 33.9% N 類股，若其可轉債全數轉股將升⾄
23.8% 、 48.2% ；亞⾺遜與 Google 兩⼤客戶合計持股逾 28% 。

### Page 6

4   招股⽂件顯⽰， 2026 年 1 ⾄ 9 ⽉營收 332.4 億美元，第三季 169.6 億美元， 10 ⽉底年化營收約 810
億美元；同期營業虧損 12.6 億美元，帳⾯淨損多數為⾮現⾦評價調整。
5   以價格區間計，隱含市值約 9,175 億⾄ 1.02 兆美元； Altimeter 執⾏⻑表⽰已申請 50 億美元配售
額度，富時羅素通知最快可於 12/11 納⼊羅素 1000 指數。
三⼤結論
1  AI 模型業者⾸度以公開市場定價，⽤量成⻑能否抵銷 Token （計費單位）降價，將直接影響市
場對 AI 終端變現的信⼼，並回頭牽動上游算⼒需求的評價。
2   年化營收不等於已實現收⼊，帳⾯虧損也含⼤量⾮現⾦項⽬，評價須拆解使⽤量、單位收費、
算⼒成本與股權稀釋；⼤客戶交叉持股使營收與雲端資本⽀出⾼度連動。
3   當掛牌後付費留存與⽑利改善、算⼒⽀出占營收⽐續降時，應以正向看待 AI 軟體與上游算⼒需
求；若 Token 價格跌幅快於⽤量成⻑或上市後估值劇烈修正，應降低對模型與軟體⾼估值部位的

容忍度。
事件  7
11/16 Fed 主席華許提出 AI 三種經濟情境，納⼊脆弱性評估
五⼤重點
1  Fed 主席華許 11/16 為舊⾦⼭ Fed 活動準備的講稿表⽰， Fed 幕僚已開始推演 AI 加速普及對美國
經濟的不同情境，並強調這些情境不是預測。
2   第⼀種情境是 AI 推升⽣產⼒與成⻑、由雇主與勞⼯共享繁榮，第⼆種是經濟活動增加的好處被
⼤規模失業抵銷；第三種是技術不如預期，引發股價下跌與經濟放緩。
3   華許稱 Fed 的⾓⾊不是⽀持或反對 AI ，⽽是評估 AI 可能帶來的潛在脆弱性，包括遭不良⾏為者
利⽤造成擾亂，以及對勞動市場的重⼤衝擊。
4  Fed 主席把「 AI 不如預期導致股市下跌」列為正式情境，代表 AI 投資回落對⾦融市場與景氣的
衝擊，已被納⼊央⾏的政策評估範圍。
5   講稿未對 12 ⽉利率決策表態； Fed 10 ⽉以 10 ⽐ 2 維持利率 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% ，⽬前利率期貨已幾
乎完全定價 12 ⽉升息⼀碼，短期政策仍由通膨數據主導。

三⼤結論

### Page 7

1  Fed 把 AI 泡沫破裂列為情境，意味 AI 資本⽀出若未能變現，貨幣政策可能同時⾯對股價修正與
景氣放緩； AI 相關資產的評價不能只看需求成⻑，也要看現⾦回收。
2   ⼤規模失業情境將改變 Fed 對就業⽬標的判斷，⽣產⼒情境則可能降低通膨壓⼒；這是⻑期框
架，短期利率仍以核⼼通膨與就業數據為依據。
3   當 AI 企業⾃由現⾦流持續為負、融資成本上升且變現覆蓋度下滑時，應降低⾼槓桿雲端與⾼估
值 AI 衛星；若雲端營收持續加速並帶動現⾦流改善，才提⾼ AI 供應鏈的⾵險權重。
事件  8
11/17 歐盟執委會秋季預測：法國今年⾚字 5.5% ，明年成⻑下修⾄ 0.6%
五⼤重點
1   歐盟執委會 11/17 公布秋季經濟預測，歐元區 2026 與 2027 年 GDP 均成⻑ 1.2% 、 2028 年 1.3% ；
2026 年通膨預估 3.2% ， 2027 年 3.1% ， 2028 年才回落⾄ 2.1% 。
2   法國 2026 年財政⾚字預估擴⼤⾄ GDP 的 5.5% ，⾼於前次預測 5.1% 與政府⽬標 5.4% ； 2027 年

僅⼩降⾄ 5.4% ，仍遠⾼於 5% ⽬標， 2027 年政府債務由 120.2% 上修⾄ 122.1% 。
3   執委會說明，法國經濟成⻑低迷與公債利息⽀出增加，將抵銷 2027 年預算中的⼤規模財政整
頓；法國 2027 年成⻑預測由 1.1% ⼤幅下修⾄ 0.6% 。
4   德國 2027 年成⻑預測由 0.9% 上修⾄ 1.0% 、⻄班⽛由 1.9% 升⾄ 2.1% 、義⼤利由 0.6% 升⾄
0.7% ；歐元區整體⾚字將由 2025 年 3.0% 升⾄ 2026 年 3.5% 。
5   法國 CAC 40 指數 11/17 收跌 1.62% ⾄ 8,216.78 點；同⽇法國爆發新⼀輪⽰威，內政部統計全國
約 14.6 萬⼈參與，政治與財政壓⼒仍未緩解。
三⼤結論
1   歐元區通膨預估到 2027 年仍⾼於 3% ，⽀持 ECB 維持緩步升息路徑；成⻑僅約 1.2% ，政策緊縮
與能源成本將持續壓抑企業獲利，景氣擴張但⼒道有限。
2   法國⾚字與債務上修、成⻑下修，是區域中最脆弱的⼀環；德國與南歐預測反⽽上修，主權⾵

險未全⾯擴散，不宜把法國壓⼒外推為全歐危機。
3   當法國預算審議再陷僵局、法德利差擴⼤時，應降低法國內需與主權債曝險；歐洲股市維持中
性偏審慎，偏好低負債、具海外收⼊與成本轉嫁能⼒的公司，待 PMI 廣度與能源改善再增加週期
曝險。

11/17 美銀經理⼈調查：科技股淨超配⽐例降⾄ 12% ，創 2006 年來最⼤降幅
五⼤重點
1   美國銀⾏ 11/17 公布 11 ⽉全球基⾦經理⼈調查（ 11/6 ⾄ 11/12 進⾏），科技股淨超配⽐例由 10 ⽉
的 37% 降⾄ 12% ，為 2006 年 6 ⽉以來最⼤降幅。
2   創紀錄的淨 38% 受訪者認為企業過度投資 AI ， 52% 預期未來六個⽉將有⼤型雲端業者宣布削減
資本⽀出；淨 32% 預期全球 CPI （消費者物價指數）未來 12 個⽉下降。
3   美銀策略師哈特尼特表⽰，市場對 AI 繁榮的狂熱已經落幕，投資⼈強烈呼籲企業暫緩 AI 相關投
⼊。
4   調查期間涵蓋 11/12 輝達換帥前後；經理⼈⼤舉減碼科技，與輝達 11/17 公布營收年增 103% 、
Rubin 量產的基本⾯形成明顯落差。
5   費城半導體指數 11/17 盤中⼀度跌逾 3% ，終場收漲 0.34% ⾄ 9,642.88 點，仍較 52 週⾼點 14,655

點低約 34% （推算），科技股已⼤幅修正。
三⼤結論
1   經理⼈⼤幅減碼科技反映對 AI 資本⽀出回報的疑慮，⽽⾮已確認的需求下滑；上游財報與出貨
仍強，情緒與基本⾯的落差可能放⼤科技股的雙向波動。
2   若⼤型雲端業者真的削減資本⽀出，衝擊將先落在依賴外部融資的新雲端與中游組裝；具定價
權與現⾦流的晶圓代⼯、記憶體⿓頭受影響相對較⼩。
3   當雲端業者下⼀季財報確認資本⽀出維持、且科技持股已降⾄低檔時，可分批增加⾼品質上
游；若有⼤型雲端業者正式下修資本⽀出，應先降低 ODM 、⾼槓桿雲端與⾼估值軟體部位。

11/17 京東第三季營收年增 3.5% 、淨利 65 億元，美股 ADR 收跌 7.66%

3   京東港股 11/17 收跌 2.68% ⾄ 105.2 港元，美股 ADR 收跌 7.66% ⾄ 25.43 美元，接近 52 週低點

不等於消費需求回溫，股價反應偏弱顯⽰市場更重視營收動能。
2   中國 10 ⽉零售與固定投資轉弱，政策尚未修復居⺠收⼊與資產價格；中國股市宜維持選擇性
衛星，優先財務健全的出⼝技術製造，內需平台不宜全⾯提⾼權重。
3   當中國零售、⺠間投資與居⺠收⼊連續改善、平台營收重回雙位數成⻑時，才考慮提⾼中國內
需股權重；若零售持續低於 1% 且價格競爭延續，應維持低配並以已知⽀出匹配⼈⺠幣部位。

待確認
1. 推算值：事件 3 「汽⾞及零件銷售下滑」由整體與排除汽⾞增幅推算；事件 5 兩⽇累計跌點與跌幅；事件 6 新股募資
約 593 億美元；事件 9 費半距 52 週⾼點跌幅；事件 10 營收增加額。
2. 單⼀來源或⼆⼿來源：事件 1 市場預期 1,088 億美元、盤後漲幅為媒體與報價資料；事件 2 利率期貨機率與 10 年期殖
利率 5.10% 為市場報價，威廉斯為論壇談話⾮官⽅講稿；事件 6 營收、虧損、隱含市值與私募⾦額取⾃招股⽂件的第
三⽅整理，尚未直接核對 SEC 原⽂；事件 7 華許講稿內容取⾃快訊，未取得 Fed 官網全⽂；事件 9 為美銀調查轉述。
3. 事件 6 Anthropic 預計 11/18 定價、 11/19 交易，最終價格與規模尚未確定；事件 1 管理層在法說會提及的年度成⻑⽬
標未取得逐字稿，未寫⼊。
4. 尚未公布：英國 10 ⽉ CPI （ 11/18 ）、 10 ⽉ FOMC 會議紀要（ 11/19 ）、台灣 10 ⽉外銷訂單（ 11/20 ）、川普訪華與

11/19 深圳三⽅會晤結果、德國內閣對 ECB ⼈事的決定。
5. 與上⼀輪⽐對（「全球⼗⼤財經事件｜ 2026/11/17 07:51 （台灣時間）」）：已列之台股 11/16 重挫、美國商務部遠端
算⼒管制、美股 11/16 科技股重挫、哈瑪克談話、紐約州製造業指數、布蘭特 11/16 下跌、中國 10 ⽉社零與⼯業增加
值、⽇本第三季 GDP 、中國證實三⽅會晤、英國 2 年期殖利率觸及 5% ，本輪均未再列為事件；事件 10 僅以⼀句背景
提及中國 10 ⽉社零。
6. 新事實晚於上⼀輪資料同步時間（ 11/17 07:51 ）：事件 4 美軍空襲格什姆島（台灣時間 11/17 23:55 起）、事件 5 台股
11/17 收盤（台灣時間 11/17 13:30 ），均以新事實為主體；事件 1 輝達財報與上⼀輪的股價事件屬不同公告。
7. 72 ⼩時內無主要央⾏實際調整利率或準備⾦率；⼈⾏外匯存款準備⾦率 11/15 ⽣效已於 11/16 07:51 報告列出。
8. 48 ⾄ 72 ⼩時⼊選 0 件： 11/15 ⾄ 11/16 上午的事件多已於上⼀輪列出，其餘份量低於較新事件。

9. 九⼤分類中，台灣經濟與央⾏近 72 ⼩時無重⼤官⽅數據或央⾏動作；⽇本經濟與央⾏僅有前⽇銀總裁⿊⽥與經濟
財政⼤⾂城內實的原則性談話，份量不⾜；歐洲、⽇本公司無⾜夠份量的財報或公告；中美國際政治經濟競爭⽅⾯，
川普訪華與深圳會晤尚未舉⾏，故未單獨成件。

烈⾏情⽰警（事件 5 ）、⼤型公司財報（事件 1 、 10 ）、 AI 重要發展（事件 1 、 6 、 7 、 9 ）。

### Page 1

十一月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 金融 ‧ 台灣

中信金 
(2891.TW/2891 TT)

前 10月調整後獲利年增121%，提升 2026F配息能力 
重要訊息   
中信金前 10 月每股盈餘年增 1.8%至 3.45 元，但若加計 FVOCI 股票資本利得
之調整後每股盈餘則年增 121%至 7.57 元。 
評論及分析 
前 10 月調整後獲利年增幅度擴大至 121%。中信金 10 月稅後淨利月減
43%至 53.1 億元，帶動前 10 月稅後淨利年增 1.8%至 686 億元，達凱基原
先全年預估 82%。但若加計銀行、壽險之 FVOCI 股票資本利得 803 億元，
中信金前 10 月調整後淨利為 1,489 億元，年增 121%，已達凱基先前全年
預估 118%。其中前 10 月中信銀獲利年增 18%(淨利息收入及手續費收入各
年增約 20%，抵銷呆帳年增 20 億元)，抵銷台壽保稅後淨利年減 27%。 
2026F 可分配盈餘超過 2,000 億元。管理層先前表示 2026F 現金股利配發
仍需考量明年各業務成長規劃及壽險上繳獲利需視主管機關規定。由於
3Q26 中信金每股淨值季增 3.3%至 35.55 元，凱基推估其他權益仍維持超
過 700 億元水準。若年底仍其他權益維持正數，則先前提列特別盈餘公積
315 億元將迴轉至 2026F 可分配盈餘，預估 2026F 可分配盈餘將超過 2,000
億元水準。凱基下修 2026F 稅後淨利 1.3%，但上修調整後獲利 30%，反
映前 10 月調整後獲利優於預期，並上修 2026F 中信金每股現金股利 3-3.25
元(約調整後每股盈餘之 36-40%)，高於目前市場平均 2.8 元，預計現金股
利配發來源由銀行、壽險及其他子公司獲利上繳、金控銀行借款，配置比例
分別為 70%、7%、8%及 15%。 
預估 2027F 銀行核心獲利成長。由於美國重啟升息帶動美台利差擴大，加
上中信銀北美、OBU 及日本之外幣放款將受惠台灣供應鏈移轉，將有助提升
中信銀 2027F 外幣放款維持高個數成長，凱基預估 2027F 中信銀調整後淨
利差有機會維持 2026 之 1.7-1.73%水準，2027F 淨利息收入維持中個位數
成長。此外，管理層表示若台灣央行每升息 12.5 基點，將提升中信銀淨利
差約 0.9 基點。此外，由於中信銀前 10 月財管手收年增率達 30%，凱基預
估 2027F 財管手收至少維持中個位數成長，反映 2027F 台灣經常帳順差擴
大帶動超額儲蓄成長至 11 兆元，資金活水有助維持銀行財管動能。 
投資建議 
凱基預估 2027F 中信銀外幣放款成長將持續受惠台灣供應鏈移轉，帶動
2027F 淨利息收入年增維持至少中個位數，手續費收入維持中個位數成長，
加上 2026F 可分配盈餘充沛提升中信金每股現金股利上看 3-3.25 元，現金
股利殖利率 4%-4.3%，投資建議維持「增加持股」，目標價 84 元，基於總
和評量法，約當 2027F 股價淨值比 1.9 倍。 
投資風險 
全球金融市場轉弱；銀行信用成本大幅上揚。 
增加持股‧維持

圖 20: 公司概況  圖 21: 組織結構圖 
中國信託金控成立於 2002 年 5 月，為以銀行為主體之金控公
司，2022年 9 月資產達 2,522 億美元，台灣 16家金控中排名第
3。 中信金旗下涵蓋的金融業務範圍橫跨銀行、證券、保險、投
信、保險經紀、創投與彩劵業務等，中信銀同時亦是國內銀行中
最國際化金融機構，目前中信銀除在台灣擁有 147 家分行，中信
銀海外子行包括美國、加拿大、印尼、菲律賓及 2014 年 6 月購
併之日本東京之星銀行，4Q21 取得泰國 LHFG 集團增持股至
46.6%，成為 LHFG 集團最大股東，海外分行包括香港、新加
坡、大陸、印度、泰國及越南。中信銀為台灣消金業務龍頭，在
財富管理業務及信用卡業務均取得領先地位，在衍生性金融商品
交易亦居首位
